# Context: system prompt assembly, compaction, instruction files and memory

Round 2 synthesis, group 07. Research date 2026-11-10. Source code of thirteen harnesses, each pinned to one commit (`research/round2/00-repos.tsv`). Every claim carries a trust label: `checked in review` (an independent reviewer opened the cited code), `corrected in review` (the text is the reviewer's corrected version), `traced end to end` or `read the definition` (the investigator's own confidence, not re-checked), `unchecked`, or `inferred`. A negative claim ("there is no X") says `absence confirmed` only if a reviewer searched for it.

## 1. Scope

**Group.** How the system prompt and tool descriptions are assembled and kept cache-stable; compaction (summarising old context) in full; pruning of old tool output; how compaction is recorded in the log; instruction-file discovery (AGENTS.md and its relatives); memory and session search.

**Files read.** For each repository the findings that belong to this group, wherever they sit, plus that file's sections 4 (Round 1 claims), 6 (not settled) and 8 (review). Per repository: `codex.agents.md` and `codex.sessions.md`; `opencode.agents.md` and `opencode.sessions.md`; `openclaw.agents.md`, `.sessions.md`, `.safety.md`; `hermes-agent.agents.md`, `.sessions.md`, `.safety.md` (threat-pattern scan); `oh-my-pi.agents.md`, `.sessions.md`; `pi.agents.md`, `.sessions.md`, `.safety.md`; `deepseek-harness.agents.md`, `.sessions.md`; `goose.agents.md`, `.sessions.md`, `.safety.md`; `cline.agents-sessions.md`, `cline.safety.md`; `openhands.agents-sessions.md`, `openhands.safety.md`; `zed.md`; `warp.md`; `opencodex.md`. Review coverage differs a lot per project and is stated in each account.

**Not available.**
- OpenHands: the repository is only the Agent Canvas web client. The condenser, prompt templates, memory and event store live in `OpenHands/software-agent-sdk`, which was not cloned. Only client-side configuration and the `Condensation` event shape are known.
- Warp: the agent loop, prompt and summarisation run on Warp's server; the wire schema repository (`warpdotdev/warp-proto-apis`) was not read.
- OpenCodex: a gateway with no agent loop. The calling client owns history, prompt, instruction files and memory.
- Zed: the study scope covered compaction and output limits only; instruction (rules) files and memory are not covered. Pi has no memory feature in the files read.
- Some compaction internals were not read: Oh My Pi's `prepareCompaction`, its prompts and the `handoff`, `shake` and `soft` methods; Hermes' gateway "hygiene" compaction and micro-compaction; OpenClaw's log record of a compaction.

**Citations.** All permalinks were copied from the input files and pass `research/round2/check_citations.py` (path exists, range inside the file). Two OpenCode ranges in section 2 (`compaction.ts` L872-L885, the plugin-hook summary path, and L892-L906, usage recording) are not in any input file; I opened the clone and confirmed they hold what the text says.

**Terms.** *Window*: the model's context size in tokens. *Compaction*: replacing old history with a summary. *Summariser*: the model call that writes the summary. *Cache-preserving summary request*: a summary request that begins with exactly the bytes of the main conversation's request, so the provider's prefix cache is hit. *Prune*: shrink or drop old tool output without a summary. *Baseline*: the first rendered value of an instruction source in a session; later changes are sent as diffs.

## 2. Implementations by project

Order: Codex, OpenCode, OpenClaw, Hermes Agent, Oh My Pi, Pi, DeepSeek harness, goose, Cline, OpenHands, Zed, Warp, OpenCodex. Each account ends with the project's row for the comparison, its Round 1 rows and its open points, which are repeated in sections 3, 5 and 7.

### Codex

Repository `openai/codex`, branch `main`, pinned `82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053`, Rust (`codex-rs/`). Sources: `codex.agents.md` (sections 3.1, 3.3, 3.4 and 8; the review checked 17 of 29 findings) and `codex.sessions.md` (resume and compaction base). The review did not check world-state diffing, tool-list assembly, local and remote compaction beyond the trigger, the memory read path or the ad-hoc note tool.

#### Prompt assembly and cache

- **Base prompt is catalog data.** The model's system prompt is `ModelInfo.model_messages.instructions_template`, a string inside the bundled `models.json` (one per model; the catalog can also be refreshed from a backend, 300 s cache). `models-manager/prompt.md` (275 lines) is used only for an unknown model slug. `model_instructions_file` replaces the template; `personality = none` strips the `# Personality` section by text surgery. A model with no template runs with empty instructions and a warning. Several legacy `gpt_5_*_prompt.md` files are referenced nowhere (dead). Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/prompts/src/model_instructions.rs#L8-L17 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/models-manager/src/model_info.rs#L46-L56 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/models-manager/src/model_info.rs#L151-L156 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/models-manager/models.json#L76-L76 . Trust: traced end to end (unchecked in review). Whether a server-side catalog refresh can change prompt text without a client release is inferred; the refresh endpoint was not read.
- **Everything else is "world state" sections, diffed per step.** `build_world_state_for_step` builds typed sections each step: tool declarations (in incremental mode), model instructions, AGENTS.md, permissions (sandbox mode, approval policy, exec-policy prefixes, writable roots), collaboration mode, environment (cwd, date, shell, sub-agent roster), apps, plugins, deferred tools, multi-agent hints, model catalog, managed requirements. Each section has a stable persisted id and a snapshot. `record_step_world_state_if_changed` renders each section against the persisted baseline, records only changed fragments as developer or user messages, and persists a `WorldState` rollout item. The diff is a JSON merge-patch where `null` means delete, so a section must never serialise to null. AGENTS.md changes render as "These AGENTS.md instructions replace all previously provided AGENTS.md instructions." or a removal notice. Fresh windows (new thread, after compaction) render everything. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/world_state.rs#L262-L262 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/mod.rs#L4180-L4207 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/mod.rs#L5096-L5130 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/context/world_state/mod.rs#L343-L348 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/context/world_state/agents_md.rs#L58-L75 . Trust: traced end to end (unchecked in review). Cost noted by the investigator: many special cases (legacy-fragment and retained-fragment matchers).
- **Model switch** emits a `ModelSwitchInstructions` developer fragment placed before all other developer context (https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/mod.rs#L5264-L5270 ). Provenance of the prompt (`Model{slug}` or `Custom`) is recorded at session start.
- **Tool list is rebuilt every step** from (model row, feature flags, environment): `build_tool_router` makes a registry; `exec_command` plus `write_stdin` with unified exec; `apply_patch` only when the model row sets `apply_patch_tool_type`; `update_plan` only when enabled; collaboration tools when multi-agent is on; MCP, extension and hosted tools appended, and a "tool exposure policy" can move some behind a BM25 `tool_search`. `tool_mode` in the model row overrides feature flags: 11 of 12 bundled models carry `code_mode_only` although the feature flag is off (Round 1 corrected). In "incremental tools" mode the top-level `tools` list holds only hosted tools and function declarations travel in history as a world-state section. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/tools/spec_plan.rs#L122-L135 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/tools/mod.rs#L66-L76 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs#L1850-L1871 . Trust: traced end to end for registration (unchecked in review).
- **The sub-agent tool description is the delegation prompt.** `spawn_agent`'s description is built in Rust and carries the policy: do not spawn unless the user or AGENTS.md or a skill explicitly asks; depth or thoroughness requests do not count; disjoint write sets; call `wait_agent` sparingly. It is advisory; the code gate is the depth and thread limits. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/tools/handlers/multi_agents_spec.rs#L692-L772 . Trust: checked in review.

#### Compaction

1. **Triggers.** After each sampling step the loop computes a context-window status. The auto-compact limit is `min(model_auto_compact_token_limit, 0.9 x window)` (a 400,000 window gives a 360,000 limit in the tests); the full window is `window x effective_context_window_percent` (serde default 95). Scope `total` counts all active tokens; scope `body_after_prefix` subtracts the window's prefill baseline, so a large injected prefix does not count. Three phases: pre-turn (before the new input is recorded), mid-turn (when tool calls need a follow-up and the limit is hit), and post-turn (only when `model_post_turn_compact_threshold_percent` is above 0; default 0 = off). A model downshift or changed "compaction hash" compacts against the previous model first. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/context_window.rs#L62-L120 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/protocol/src/openai_models.rs#L568-L590 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/protocol/src/openai_models.rs#L2143-L2153 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs#L1548-L1575 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs#L745-L783 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs#L866-L912 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs#L1592-L1674 . Trust: checked in review. A pre-turn failure preserves and records the incoming user input and surfaces the error; a post-turn failure is logged and the completed turn is kept (https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs#L261-L311 ).
2. **Size measurement.** The input files describe the status computation (active-token usage against the buffered limit) but not how usage is obtained; no estimator is described. Not settled.
3. **Local compaction (providers without remote compaction).** The harness appends a fixed user message ("You are performing a CONTEXT CHECKPOINT COMPACTION. Create a handoff summary for another LLM that will resume the task.", four bullets: progress and decisions, constraints and preferences, remaining steps, critical data) to a clone of the history, samples the same turn model with no tools (`Prompt::default()` plus `base_instructions`) and takes the last assistant message as the summary. The prompt is overridable with `compact_prompt`. The request is the full history plus one appended user message, so it shares the main prefix, but the findings do not describe it as designed for cache reuse (trust: inferred). Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/prompts/templates/compact/prompt.md#L1-L9 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs#L127-L133 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs#L264-L281 . Trust: traced end to end (unchecked beyond the trigger).
4. **What is kept.** New history = re-injected initial context, then the most recent real user messages up to 20,000 tokens (newest first, oldest truncated), then one user message `SUMMARY_PREFIX + summary`. Tool calls and outputs, assistant messages and developer messages are not carried (except explicitly marked client-authored retained tool outputs and pending inter-agent mail). An empty summary becomes "(no summary available)" in some paths. PreCompact and PostCompact hooks can stop compaction. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs#L383-L396 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs#L729-L805 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs#L820-L824 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs#L665-L712 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs#L198-L246 . Trust: traced end to end.
5. **Remote compaction V2 (default for the OpenAI provider and Azure Responses).** The client sends the normal prompt plus a trailing `CompactionTrigger` item and requires exactly one opaque `Compaction` item back (anything else is fatal). Client-side retained items (user messages, hook prompts, non-progress agent messages under a cap, retained client tool outputs) are trimmed oldest-first to a 64,000-token budget. Stream retries inside compaction are capped at 2 (5 for sampling). The summary is opaque and cannot be read or edited. A background ("async") variant that prepares a candidate while the turn runs, and a "token budget" mode that starts a fresh window without summarising, both exist and are default off. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/model-provider/src/capabilities.rs#L33-L58 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact_remote_v2.rs#L478-L482 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact_remote_v2.rs#L494-L563 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact_remote_v2.rs#L93-L96 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/async_compaction.rs#L42-L52 . Trust: read the definition (request side and retention); unchecked beyond the trigger.
6. **Atomic replace.** History is replaced only after the compaction call returns; a failed attempt leaves history unchanged and usage is recomputed after replacement. Review correction: for post-turn compaction an empty summary is an error, but for the other phases `unwrap_or_default()` accepts an empty summary and the history is still replaced. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs#L439-L455 . Trust: corrected in review.
7. **Recovery from a context-length error: three behaviours.** (a) Inside compaction: on `ContextWindowExceeded`, drop the oldest history item and retry (counter reset) until one item remains, then mark the window full and fail. (b) In a normal sampling request: not retried; the session marks its token usage full (so the next turn's pre-sampling check compacts), emits the error and the turn ends. (c) For reviewer (guardian) sessions only: one compaction and one retry per step. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs#L347-L359 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs#L2028-L2031 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/mod.rs#L5875-L5881 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs#L914-L942 . Trust: corrected in review (atomic replace confirmed; "only after a summary exists" holds for post-turn only). Transient errors in compaction follow the sampling retry policy and honour `Retry-After`.
8. **Summariser window too small / check that it shrank.** The same model summarises, so the window is the same except after a model downshift (compact on the previous model first). The oldest-first drop in item 7(a) is the only input-size reduction described. No check that the new history is smaller is described (trust: not described in the input; no reviewer searched).

#### Pruning of old tool output

None after recording. Every function and custom tool output is middle-cut (head and tail, header "Warning: truncated output (original token count: N)") when recorded into live history; the limit is the model row's `truncation_policy` (bundled `tokens: 10000`) or `tool_output_token_limit`, with a 20% serialisation allowance. The full payload stays in the rollout. Old outputs vanish only at compaction. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/context_manager/history.rs#L658-L680 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/utils/output-truncation/src/lib.rs#L38-L50 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/models-manager/models.json#L15-L18 . Trust: checked in review.

#### Record in the log

The rollout is an append-only JSONL file. A compaction item carries `replacement_history` (a complete base) and a token-usage snapshot. Resume picks the newest surviving compaction with a replacement history as the base ("older rollout items do not affect rebuilt history") and replays only the suffix, scanning backwards; `ThreadRolledBack` markers drop the newest N user turns. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/session/rollout_reconstruction.rs#L205-L260 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/history/src/lib.rs#L318-L336 . Trust: read the definition (sessions file, unchecked). Because replacement is atomic and the item is written after the summary exists, a failure leaves the log without a compaction item (inferred from the atomic-replace finding).

#### Instruction files

- **Discovery.** The project root is the nearest ancestor with a marker (default `.git`; `project_root_markers` are read from non-project config layers only, so a repository cannot move its own root). From root to cwd, each directory contributes at most one file: the first existing of `AGENTS.override.md`, `AGENTS.md`, then configured fallback names (names with path separators, `.`, `..` or NUL are ignored). Files are concatenated root first. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/agents_md.rs#L199-L270 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/agents_md.rs#L207-L212 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/agents_md.rs#L279-L303 . Trust: checked in review.
- **Budget.** `project_doc_max_bytes` default 32 KiB, combined across all project files (one `remaining` counter). The file that crosses it is truncated, later files are skipped, each with a warning; unused budget is not refilled. Whitespace-only files do not consume budget, but an empty `AGENTS.override.md` still shadows `AGENTS.md` in its directory. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/agents_md.rs#L69-L76 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/agents_md.rs#L148-L182 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/config/src/config_toml.rs#L77-L77 . Trust: checked in review.
- **Global file and cache.** `$CODEX_HOME/AGENTS.override.md`, else `AGENTS.md` (an empty override falls through here, unlike the project rule); not counted against the 32 KiB (a warning only). Thread-level host instructions are capped at 10,000 estimated tokens and rejected, not cut. The project part is cached keyed by (environment selection, trust level, byte budget), so a mid-session edit is not re-read (inferred from the key; not run). Text is injected as a user-role fragment `# AGENTS.md instructions ... </INSTRUCTIONS>`, not a system message; the base prompt tells the model to read nested AGENTS.md below cwd on demand. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/codex-home/src/instructions/mod.rs#L38-L80 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/agents_md_manager.rs#L83-L106 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/agents_md_manager.rs#L199-L212 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/context/user_instructions.rs#L28-L32 . Trust: read the definition; caching inferred.
- **Trust and failure.** An untrusted project (`active_project.is_untrusted()`) or a zero budget returns nothing (https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/agents_md.rs#L65-L67 ). With a sandboxed read policy a read error is fatal to loading; without a sandbox it is logged and ignored (https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/agents_md.rs#L104-L117 ). Trust: checked in review.

#### Memory

- **Pipeline (feature `memories`, stable, default off).** A background task starts with a root, non-ephemeral session: prune old data; skip if rate limits are low (default below 25% remaining). Phase 1 claims up to 2 rollouts that have been idle 6 hours and are under 10 days old from the SQLite state DB (scan limit 5,000, one-hour lease, concurrency 8) and asks an extraction model to produce a summary and slug per rollout. Phase 2 takes one global lock, syncs `raw_memories.md` and `rollout_summaries/` into `$CODEX_HOME/memories(_v2)`, computes a git diff against the last successful baseline, and only if something changed spawns an internal consolidation agent that rewrites `MEMORY.md`, `memory_summary.md` and `skills/`. That agent runs with approvals `Never`, no network, write access only to the memory root, no collaboration, no memory tool, no apps, plugins or MCP, and is ephemeral so it cannot feed Phase 1. If the parent profile is not a managed sandbox, the consolidation agent inherits that unsandboxed profile, so "memory root only" holds only when the parent is sandboxed. Jobs are leased with retry backoff. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/memories/write/src/start.rs#L24-L45 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/memories/write/src/phase1.rs#L133-L150 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/memories/write/src/phase2.rs#L306-L373 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/memories/write/src/phase2.rs#L339-L359 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/features/src/lib.rs#L1445-L1450 . Trust: checked in review (gates and phase 2 profile).
- **Who writes, approval.** A background model writes; no person reviews it. No ledger or rollback is described, but the memory root is a git-diffed tree (the git baseline is used as a change gate, not as an undo; trust: inferred that it could serve as one).
- **Read path.** At each thread-context build, `memory_summary.md` is read, truncated to 2,500 tokens, wrapped with the memory folder path and injected as developer fragments (V2: split into 8,900-byte pieces). The model reads further files with ordinary file tools (or dedicated list, read and search tools when `dedicated_tools` is on). `redact_secrets` runs on the rollout sent to the extraction model and again on its output (pattern pass; rules not read). `disable_on_external_context` marks a thread "memory mode polluted" when a response item may include MCP or web-search content, excluding it from extraction (not from reading). Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/ext/memories/src/prompts.rs#L28-L58 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/ext/memories/src/lib.rs#L16-L16 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/memories/write/src/phase1.rs#L397-L424 , https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/core/src/stream_events_utils.rs#L176-L192 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked).
- **Chat-time notes.** With `memories.dedicated_tools` on (default off), the chat agent can add an ad-hoc note: a new file under `extensions/ad_hoc/notes/` created with `create_new` (no overwrite), symlinks rejected, filename 128 and slug 80 bytes at most, no approval step beyond the ordinary tool policy; Phase 2 later folds it in. The investigator calls this a prompt-injection path into persistent memory. Trace: https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/82e6bacb9c6950d3b56cb153847fa80bdd698053/codex-rs/ext/memories/src/local/ad_hoc_note.rs#L12-L47 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked).
- **Session search.** Not in the findings read for this group (the SQLite state DB indexes rollouts for the memory pipeline; no content search tool is described).

### OpenCode

Repository: `anomalyco/opencode`, branch `dev`, commit `05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f`. All of this is the V2 engine in `packages/core/src`. Source files: `opencode.agents.md` (findings and review table read in full for this group), `opencode.sessions.md` (log and fork findings). Review checked 19 of 38 agents findings; none needed a text correction.

#### Prompt assembly and cache

- The system prompt is two system parts: the base prompt (the agent's own `system` text, else `runner/prompt/system.txt`, 15 lines) and a keyed "instructions" block. The base prompt has a `${OPENCODE_TOOL_GUIDANCE}` slot filled from the names of the tools actually advertised (shell, write, edit paragraphs). Empty parts are dropped. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/model-request.ts#L105-L128 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/system-prompt.ts#L5-L28 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/runner/prompt/system.txt#L1-L15 . Trust: traced end to end.
- Per-model overlays live in six text files under `plugin/system-prompt/`. A plugin hook on `context`, `compaction` and `generate` requests picks one by substring of the model id (`gpt`, `claude` as a 300-byte append, `kimi`, `trinity`, `muse`); every other model gets the generic prompt. An agent that defines `system` skips the overlay. Errors are swallowed. Matching has no provider check, so a gateway-served model whose id contains "gpt" gets the GPT prompt. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/plugin/optimize.ts#L16-L30 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/plugin/optimize.ts#L51-L62 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/plugin/optimize.ts#L78-L104 . Trust: checked in review.
- A "# Your Model" identity block is spliced in at index 1 for the same request kinds, so it is constant within a session and changes only on a model switch. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/plugin/identity.ts#L10-L31 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked).
- Dynamic state is kept out of the cached prompt by "instruction sources". Each contextual item (Code Mode catalog, MCP guidance, references, skill list, AGENTS.md files, date and environment, API-attached entries) is a keyed source with a JSON value and renderers for initial, changed and removed. Per session the runner hashes each canonical-JSON value and publishes a durable `session.instructions.updated` event holding the hash delta and the already-rendered update text (frozen in the event, so it is never re-rendered). The first observation is the baseline and is rendered into `system[1]` on every request. Later changes become a `system` message appended to history at the moment of change ("The instructions from X changed. Here's the diff"). Sources are ordered from most to least shared across sessions. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/context.ts#L159-L175 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/instructions/index.ts#L143-L160 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/instruction-state.ts#L49-L89 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/message-updater.ts#L154-L166 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/runner/llm.ts#L225-L232 . Trust: traced end to end.
- Date and environment are sources too: the date changes daily and its change is a mid-conversation system message, so the cached prefix survives midnight. The skill list is a keyed `<available_skills>` block filtered by permission rules, with no budget in that file. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/instructions/builtins.ts#L22-L53 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/skill/instructions.ts#L21-L46 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/skill/instructions.ts#L62-L80 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked).
- Failure: a source that errors becomes `unavailable` and its stored value stands. A session that was never initialised with an unavailable source refuses to start (`Instructions.InitializationBlocked`). A revert that removes an update clears the fold (`InstructionState.rewind`). Trust: traced end to end.
- Cache aids: every request carries a session-affinity header and a prompt-cache key derived from the session id (`model-request.ts` L313-L328, listed under "in the code but not in the documentation"). A keep-alive `generate` ping at 90% of the cache TTL exists, off by default, and stops on a cache miss (round 1 claim "90% of TTL or 4 min, 30 min window, not for subagents" confirmed: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/plugin/warming.ts#L36-L80 ). Trust: checked in review for the claim row.
- Tool descriptions: the tool list is not described in the agents file beyond the tool-guidance slot and Code Mode. In the sessions file, tools are "Code Mode" by default: the model gets one `execute` tool plus a catalog in its instructions in which every namespace is listed and signatures are added round-robin until a budget is reached. Trust: unchecked (sessions file, not traced here beyond its summary).

#### Compaction

Trigger and measurement.
1. At the top of each step the runner calls `estimatePrompt` and then `compaction.compact({reason:"auto"})`. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/runner/llm.ts#L249-L258 .
2. `compact` returns `skipped` unless auto is on and `due()`. `due` is true when the estimate is at or above the ceiling; it is not due right after a compaction, and a native window needs a measured response first. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L301-L315 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L440-L449 .
3. The estimate is the provider-measured token count of the last assistant response plus a 4-characters-per-token estimate of what came after it. Images count 1,500 tokens and PDFs 2,000 tokens flat. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L1132-L1156 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/util/token.ts#L1-L5 . There is no self-correcting estimator beyond the measured anchor.
4. The ceiling is `window - buffer`. Default buffer is `max(15% of window, 16,000)`; the 16k floor applies only if the window is at least 32k. The buffer can be a token count or a "20%" string and is ignored if it would leave no room. The window is `min(limit.input, limit.context)`. An unknown window makes the ceiling infinite: automatic compaction never fires and only a provider overflow can trigger one. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L1163-L1186 . Trust: checked in review.
5. A failed automatic compaction fails the step (`StepFailedError`). With `auto: false` nothing compacts, not even on overflow. Usage is recorded for every attempt, including failed or interrupted ones (https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L892-L906 ).

Summariser.
- The model is chosen by `selectModel`: the hidden `compaction` agent's model if configured (`agents.compaction.model`), else the session's model. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L277-L299 . Trust: checked in review (Round 1 row).
- The request is the primary agent's normal request (same system parts, same tool definitions, same history) plus one final user message holding the summary prompt. The design comment says keeping the prefix intact lets the summary reuse the provider prompt cache. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L451-L470 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L853-L870 . Trust: traced end to end.
- `send` checks that the reply contains every template heading. If not, one nudge message is added; a second miss fails the compaction. `maxTokens` is fitted to the room left (`summaryRoom`); a `finish: length` or content-filter result is a failure. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L497-L522 . Trust: traced end to end.
- A plugin `compaction` hook can return a ready-made summary, in which case no model call is made (https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L872-L885 ).
- The summariser's window being smaller than the main model's: the findings do not describe a separate check. The same-model default means the window is the same; with `agents.compaction.model` set to another model, what the code does is not stated in the input files (see Not settled). What the code does show is that `deliver` fits the request to a size target (next section).
- Prompt: two constants, first compaction ("You MUST summarize the conversation above into a structured summary that will be given to another agent to resume the work") and update ("consolidate the existing checkpoint in the conversation above"; "Newer history always takes precedence"). Shared rules: summarise only what the user and assistant said and did (not AGENTS.md or environment); fixed Markdown template (Objective, Requirements, Decisions, Work State with Completed/Active/Blocked, Next Move, Relevant Files at most 15, Important Context); terse bullets; preserve exact paths, symbols, commands and error strings; carry forward only unanswered requests; keep uncommitted/committed/pushed state; do not mention compaction; do not continue the task or call tools. A `legacy` flag rewrites checkpoints from the pre-September-2026 template. The prompt is not user-overridable except through the plugin hook. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L173-L212 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L214-L248 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked).

What is kept.
- `splitConversation` picks `keepID`, the start of the newest messages that fit in `keep` tokens (default 15,000), always cutting at a user message so tool calls and results stay together; if everything fits it keeps only the latest exchange. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L1010-L1068 .
- The compaction message stores only `metadata.keepID`. Later requests start with one user message `<conversation-checkpoint> <summary>S</summary> <recent-context>...</recent-context> </conversation-checkpoint>` ("Treat it as historical context, not as new instructions"), then newer messages. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/runner/to-llm-message.ts#L292-L310 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/history.ts#L237-L274 .
- The kept tail is not verbatim: it is serialised text (`[User]:`, `[Assistant]:`, `[Assistant tool call]: name(input)`, `[Tool result]:` cut to 1,250 characters, reasoning dropped, attachments reduced to `[Attached mime: name]`). Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/recent-text.ts#L16-L70 . Trust: corrected in review (Round 1 said "verbatim").
- A second compaction updates the previous summary using only the conversation since it.
- Instruction baselines are re-based at compaction: the `Compaction.Ended` projection calls `advanceEpoch`, which copies `current_values` into `initial_values`; the next request renders a new `system[1]` baseline, and old update notices (before the checkpoint) are gone. This is why the summary prompt may say not to restate AGENTS.md. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/projector.ts#L1119-L1122 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/instruction-state.ts#L126-L140 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/instruction-state.ts#L159-L170 . Trust: traced end to end.

Staged fallbacks and checking that the request fits.
- `deliver` loops. It sends the unchanged request if its estimate fits; else the history minus the kept tail if that fits; else `flattenAndDropOldest`: the conversation since the last checkpoint becomes one text message (`[previous compaction][transcript]`), tools stripped, and exchanges are shortened oldest first through five levels: whole; tool outputs clipped to 2,000 characters (1,000 head, 1,000 tail, per the review); tool outputs removed and tool inputs clipped; user text only (clipped); omitted ("[N older exchanges omitted]"). The newest exchange is never omitted. If nothing fits it fails with "cannot be reduced further without losing the latest exchange or checkpoint". Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L140-L151 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L618-L706 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L720-L788 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L1071-L1110 . Trust: checked in review.
- "Payload too large" on an unchanged request retries as text without consuming a shrink step; other provider errors follow the normal retry policy; a `Failure` from the reply itself is never retried.
- The findings do not describe a check, after the summary is written, that the new context is smaller than the old one. The size check is on the summariser's input (the `deliver` ladder), not on the output. Trust: inferred from the findings (not searched for by a reviewer).

Overflow recovery.
- If the provider rejects the main request as context overflow before any output started, `recoverOverflow` calls `compact({reason:"overflow"})` once per step and the step restarts. Overflow is recognised by `isContextOverflowFailure`, built from message patterns with exclusions for rate-limit wording. An overflow after output started is not recovered and surfaces as the step error. Overflow is excluded from the generic retry path. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/runner/step.ts#L186-L197 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/runner/llm.ts#L316-L322 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L126-L127 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/ai/src/provider-error.ts#L60-L80 . Trust: checked in review.
- Inside `deliver`, each further "too long" rejection of the summary request shrinks the next attempt to 85%, 70%, 50%, then 35% of the first rejected request's size (`SHRINK_STEPS`); a rejection after that gives up. There is no deterministic non-model fallback if the summary request itself cannot be made to fit; it fails.

Background (precomputed) summary and native compaction.
- Experimental flag plus auto only. When the estimate is within 60,000 tokens (or half the cycle's room on small windows) of the ceiling, the runner forks a summary of everything before the latest response (reading that prefix from cache) and holds it in an in-process `Map` per session. At the due point it is awaited and swapped in, provided the tail is at most 40% of the budget. It is discarded if the first message, instruction baseline text, model or variant changed, `keepID` is gone, or the tail is too long; not started after two failures, when the latest response is older than the cache lifetime minus 30 s, or after a model switch. Overflow never uses it. A restart loses it and unused runs are billed. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L351-L407 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L414-L438 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L971-L976 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L265-L265 . Trust: traced end to end.
- Native compaction is a per-model setting: if `compaction.type` is `native`, the provider returns a replacement window (trigger or endpoint mechanism), stored as `native.messages` and replayed only on the same model. An encrypted native window estimates as nothing locally, so `due` waits for a measured response. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/compaction.ts#L565-L603 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/model-resolver.ts#L211-L223 . Trust: read the definition.

#### Pruning of old tool output

- There is none in V2 (absence confirmed in review: no `prune`/`elide` in the engine; review confirmed Round 1's "V1 prune ignored in V2"). Each tool result goes through `ToolOutput.truncate` once, at call time: over 2,000 lines or 50 KiB it is cut to the first lines that fit with the marker `[showing lines 1-N of M; full output saved to <path>]`; the full text goes to `<data>/tool-output/tool_<id>`, cleaned after 7 days. Limits are configurable (`tool_output.max_lines/max_bytes`). Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/tool-output.ts#L13-L15 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/tool-output.ts#L69-L120 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/runner/step.ts#L145-L152 . Trust: checked in review.
- The only other old-content reduction is `boundImages`: images over 25 MiB total are replaced oldest-first down to 15 MiB with fixed text, using hysteresis so the replaced prefix (and the cache) changes only about every 10 MiB. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/model-request.ts#L216-L244 . Trust: checked in review.

#### How compaction is recorded in the log

- Compaction is a message with `metadata.keepID` plus events (`Compaction.Ended` is projected to advance the instruction epoch). Running compaction rows are skipped when a session is forked, and a fork rewrites a compaction's `keepID` to the copied message id. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/projector.ts#L198-L250 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/projector.ts#L1119-L1122 . Trust: traced end to end (sessions file).
- The durable event log is opt-in: `Bus.configured({persist})` defaults to false and the shipped CLI server never sets it, so no row is written to the `event` table; projections (messages, parts) are the local source of truth. Only the workerd profile and SDK embedders turn it on. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/bus.ts#L186-L192 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/server/src/workerd.ts#L69-L73 . Trust: checked in review (runtime behaviour of the log endpoint inferred).
- A failure midway: a failed automatic compaction fails the step; history is otherwise unchanged by the failed summary request (the summary is only adopted when written as the compaction message). The findings do not state the write order for a crash between summary and projection. Trust: inferred.

#### Instruction files

- Candidates: `<global config>/AGENTS.md`, then every `AGENTS.md` walking up from the location to the project root (or to `$HOME` when under home), then entries of the config `instructions` array (absolute paths, `~/`, globs searched upward, http(s) URLs fetched once at config load with a 5 s timeout, last good copy kept on failure). Render order: global, project, configured. Only the name `AGENTS.md` is looked up. Each file renders as `Instructions from: <path>` plus content. Files are watched; changes are debounced 100 ms and arrive as diffs. No size limit appears in this path; an unreadable file marks the whole project source `unavailable`; access-denied files are skipped. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/config/plugin/instruction.ts#L36-L58 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/config/plugin/instruction.ts#L84-L94 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/config/plugin/instruction.ts#L151-L179 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/instruction-discovery.ts#L123-L150 . Trust: traced end to end; absence of a size limit was not confirmed (see Not settled).
- Trust: a remote instruction URL is rendered verbatim into the system block; the only guard is that it must be listed in config. No project trust prompt was found (searched `config/` and `plugin/` for "trust"; desktop or TUI may add one).
- Nested AGENTS.md: after a successful `read` inside the project, the tool walks up from the file's directory (excluding the root), collects `AGENTS.md` files not already covered, and publishes a durable synthetic message `Instructions from: <path>` with metadata `{instruction:{paths}}`. The "already injected" ledger is those synthetic messages in model-visible history since the latest completed compaction, so after compaction or revert the files are re-injected on the next read. Nested files are not watched. Trace: https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/tool/plugin/read.ts#L129-L154 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/instructions.ts#L40-L81 , https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/05d347d75054bf27778750ad5f2cd30336e81d8f/packages/core/src/session/instructions.ts#L100-L113 . Trust: traced end to end.

#### Memory and session search

- Nothing found: no memory store, memory tool, curator or session-search finding appears in `opencode.agents.md`, `opencode.sessions.md` or `opencode.safety.md` (grep for "memory" matched only unrelated in-memory caches). Skills are the nearest persistent-knowledge feature: summaries in context, body loaded by a permission-gated tool call, remote index with origin pinning and no content hash. Trust: absence not confirmed by a reviewer.

### OpenClaw

Repository: `openclaw/openclaw`, branch `main`, commit `8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881`. TypeScript on Node. Files read: `openclaw.agents.md` (compaction, memory and prompt findings, sections 4, 6, 8), `openclaw.sessions.md` (transcript storage), `openclaw.safety.md` (tool-result caps). The review checked 18 of 38 agents findings; of the findings in this group it checked workspace files, skill catalog caps, Skill Workshop, the compaction trigger, the `safeguard` default, live tool-result caps and dreaming. It did not check: system prompt assembly, tool descriptions, summary generation, overflow recovery, memory flush, pruning, memory search, who writes memory.

#### Prompt assembly and cache

- The system prompt is built in code per run (`buildAgentSystemPrompt`, string builders, no template files). The part before `SYSTEM_PROMPT_CACHE_BOUNDARY` (identity line, execution-bias and "promised work" sections, runtime context, directory roles, workspace files as "Project Context") is built under a cache keyed by `sha256` of the inputs that may change it, held in a 64-entry process map. Everything channel-, tool-, skill- and time-dependent goes after the boundary, so the prefix is identical across sessions. A trailing "Runtime" line can be relocated behind tools. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/system-prompt.ts#L570-L611 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/system-prompt.ts#L100-L121 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/system-prompt.ts#L866-L885 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked; the per-turn date/time claim was not traced).
- Three modes: `none` (one-line prompt, no skills), `minimal` (sub-agent and cron sessions, and any restricted tool allowlist), `full`. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/system-prompt.ts#L346-L354 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/run/attempt-system-prompt-prepare.ts#L250-L255 . Trust: confirmed in the Round 1 table (review table for the mode row is in section 4; the finding itself unchecked).
- Tool descriptions: the Tooling section is a hard-coded map of one-line summaries restricted to the tools actually available in this run. A "Deferred Tool Schemas" directory is appended when tool search or code mode defers schemas. Full JSON schemas and long descriptions travel in the provider's tool list, not in prose. Tools removed by policy are not shown. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/system-prompt-tool-list.ts#L20-L72 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/system-prompt-tool-list.ts#L118-L124 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/system-prompt.ts#L361-L373 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked).
- Skills: a bounded catalog (150 skills, 18,000 characters, sorted by name; falls back to a compact form with shorter descriptions to fit); bodies are read on demand through `skills_read` or `read`; the section is omitted when the model has neither tool. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/system-prompt-skills.ts#L40-L66 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/skills/loading/skill-prompt-limits.ts#L10-L40 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/system-prompt-skills.ts#L17-L23 . Trust: checked in review.
- Dynamic state is kept out of the prefix by placement (below the boundary), not by diffs. No keyed baselines or per-step diff messages were described.
- Skill Workshop: the bundled `skill-workshop` plugin is enabled by default; its autonomous mode defaults to `auto`: after a session accumulates 10 model iterations and goes idle for 30 s a background review may save or update skills; learned skills unused for 30 days are archived; each change is versioned and undoable; `propose` saves drafts instead; reviews are blocked for sessions whose key contains `acp`, `cron`, `hook` or `subagent`. No human approval by default; a person reviews only afterwards. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/skill-workshop/openclaw.plugin.json#L6-L24 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/skill-workshop/src/config.ts#L26-L32 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/skill-workshop/src/experience-review-scheduler.ts#L16-L26 . Trust: checked in review (manifest and scheduler constants only; the review prompt, the writer and the archive job were not read).

#### Compaction

Trigger and measurement.
1. After each assistant message, `AgentSession` takes the provider-reported usage as the context size (or an estimate from the last successful usage when the response was an error) and calls `shouldCompact`.
2. `shouldCompact` is true when tokens exceed `min(contextWindow - reserveTokens, floor(contextWindow * 0.75))`. The effective reserve is `max(configured, 20,000)` capped at 25% of the window, so small models do not compact constantly. Defaults: session `reserveTokens` 16,384 (before the 20,000 floor), `keepRecentTokens` 20,000, compaction enabled.
3. A separate overflow trigger fires when the provider rejects the prompt. The threshold path does not retry the prompt; the overflow path retries after compaction.
Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/packages/agent-core/src/harness/compaction/compaction-threshold.ts#L1-L14 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/packages/agent-core/src/harness/compaction/compaction.ts#L208-L222 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/sessions/agent-session-compaction.ts#L638-L695 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/agent-settings.ts#L12-L56 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/agent-compaction-constants.ts#L1-L16 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/sessions/settings-manager.ts#L127-L141 . Trust: checked in review.

Summariser and prompt.
- One model request, retried up to 3 times with 500 ms to 5 s backoff, never on caller abort, timeout or refusal. The input has tool-result `details` and runtime-context entries stripped before it reaches the model (a "SECURITY" comment in the code). Output budget is 0.8 of the reserve (0.5 for a split-turn prefix). Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/compaction.ts#L70-L125 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/packages/agent-core/src/harness/compaction/compaction.ts#L225-L296 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked).
- Prompts: structured checkpoint templates (Goal, Constraints, Progress with Done, In Progress, Blocked, Key Decisions, Next Steps, Critical Context) and an "update" variant that must preserve everything in `<previous-summary>`. The summariser system prompt tells it to attribute facts by `sender={...}` and not to continue the conversation. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/packages/agent-core/src/harness/compaction/summarization-prompts.ts#L1-L12 . Trust: read the definition.
- Model: the session's own model by default. `agents.defaults.compaction.model` overrides it, except for a session whose model selection is locked. When `compaction.model` is unset and fallbacks are configured, compaction runs through the model-fallback chain. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/compaction-runtime-context.ts#L100-L135 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/compact.ts#L424-L440 . Trust: read the definition. The Round 1 "~40k input cap" and `maxSummaryChars` 16,000 were not located (the constant `MAX_COMPACTION_SUMMARY_CHARS` is referenced but its value was not read); no summariser window check was found in the findings.

`safeguard` mode (default).
- The config default mode is `safeguard`. The hook runs before compaction: it keeps the last 3 user and assistant turns verbatim (max 12, 600 characters per message, 160 for tool results), generates a summary with required headings (`## Decisions`, `## Open TODOs`, `## Constraints/Rules`, `## Pending user asks`, `## Exact identifiers`), audits it, and regenerates once (max 3 retries) when the audit fails; if it still fails it writes a structured fallback and marks it degraded. The audit costs an extra model call per retry. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/config/defaults.ts#L478-L495 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/extensions.ts#L28-L47 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/agent-hooks/compaction-safeguard-quality.ts#L19-L42 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/agent-hooks/compaction-safeguard.ts#L79-L82 . Trust: checked in review ("quality guard on in code; schema comment says off": the Zod comment "Default: false" at https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/config/zod-schema.agent-defaults-base.ts#L157-L163 contradicts the wiring; the audit body was not traced past its constants).
- With no real conversation to summarise it writes a boundary summary instead of cancelling, so compaction does not re-trigger in a loop. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/agent-hooks/compaction-safeguard.ts#L495-L510 . Trust: read the definition (listed under "in the code but not in the documentation").
- This is the closest thing in the input to "check that the summary is acceptable", but it checks content structure and identifiers, not that the context shrank.

Overflow recovery.
1. On a provider overflow error (or a pre-flight budget prediction), refuse when the fixed context (system prompt plus tools) alone exceeds the window or the pending message alone does.
2. Stop without compacting when the error is a provider request-size ceiling.
3. Compact, at most 3 attempts (`MAX_OVERFLOW_COMPACTION_ATTEMPTS`).
4. Once per run, truncate oversized tool results in the transcript.
5. Otherwise surface a message that distinguishes "compaction failed or timed out" from "overflow".
A parked code-mode run cannot follow a session rotated by compaction, so the mid-turn continuation is withheld. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/run/overflow-context-recovery.ts#L149-L173 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/run/overflow-context-recovery.ts#L245-L257 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/run/overflow-context-recovery.ts#L279-L296 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/run/overflow-context-recovery.ts#L406-L433 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/run/overflow-context-recovery.ts#L435-L458 . Trust: traced end to end for the ladder (finding unchecked by a reviewer; the Round 1 row was confirmed).

Memory flush before compaction.
- Before a user turn the runner computes `flushThreshold = compactionThreshold - softThresholdTokens` (soft default 4,000, capped by half the window). If projected tokens exceed it, or the transcript file is 2 MiB or larger, and no flush has happened for the current compaction, it runs a hidden turn with trigger `memory`. The prompt tells the model to store durable notes only in `memory/YYYY-MM-DD.md`, treat `MEMORY.md`, `DREAMS.md`, `SOUL.md`, `AGENTS.md` as read-only, never create timestamped variants, and reply with the silent token if nothing is worth saving. The `write` tool in that run is wrapped to append to that one path only. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/memory-core/src/flush-plan.ts#L13-L38 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/memory-core/src/flush-plan.ts#L48-L91 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/auto-reply/reply/agent-runner-memory.ts#L580-L650 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/agent-tools.read.ts#L601-L640 . Trust: traced end to end (unchecked).
- Failure: if tool policy removes `write`, the flush logs a warning and the model may still report success, so memory is lost without a record; a missing provider persistence tool is a pre-inference skip (`MemoryFlushToolsUnavailableError`). Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/agent-tools.memory-flush.ts#L30-L57 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/agent-tools.memory-flush.ts#L58-L66 .

#### Pruning of old tool output

- Live cap: one tool result is capped at `min(30% of window in tokens x 4 characters, hard cap)`, hard cap 16,000 characters below a 100,000-token window, 32,000 from 100,000, 64,000 from 200,000; truncated results carry a notice telling the model to narrow arguments. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/tool-result-limits.ts#L6-L40 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/tool-result-truncation.ts#L40-L50 . Trust: checked in review.
- Cache-TTL pruning (`contextPruning.mode: "cache-ttl"`): once the provider cache TTL (default 5 minutes) has passed since the last cache touch, old tool results are projected, never within the last three assistant turns or before the first user message. Results over 4,000 characters keep the first and last 1,500 with a notice. If estimated usage (4 characters per token) is at least 50% of the window and at least 50,000 eligible characters exist (the 50,000 floor is a Round 1 correction), whole results are replaced by a placeholder until usage drops below 50%. Below 30% of the window nothing is pruned. Projections are frozen, replayed byte-identically, and recorded in a hidden transcript marker so a restart rebuilds the same prefix. Pruning changes only the prompt projection; transcript entries are not rewritten. Direct Anthropic API-key calls use server-side clearing instead. Default: off, except the Anthropic provider seeds `cache-ttl` with `ttl: "1h"` when the user left the mode unset and Anthropic auth is detected. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/tool-result-truncation.ts#L62-L89 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/tool-result-truncation.ts#L152-L173 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/tool-result-truncation.ts#L214-L281 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-runner/run/attempt-setup.ts#L288-L330 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/anthropic/config-defaults.ts#L205-L222 . Trust: read the definition and wiring (unchecked by a reviewer; the Round 1 row was confirmed).

#### How compaction is recorded in the log

- Not described in the agents findings. The sessions file gives the storage: `session_nodes` (one row per session key), `session_windows` (transcript generations of a key, each with a `reason` from `initial, reset, rollover, fork, rewind, switch, recovery, compaction` and a `previous_session_id` link), and `transcript_events` keyed `(session_id, seq)` (JSON or zstd blob, 4 MiB cap each). So a compaction can open a new generation with reason `compaction`; the pruning marker above is also a transcript entry. The sessions file states compaction itself was not read. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/state/openclaw-agent-schema.sql#L16-L51 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/state/openclaw-agent-schema.sql#L140-L171 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/state/openclaw-agent-schema.sql#L454-L505 . Trust: unchecked; the link between compaction and a new window is inferred from the enum value and the code-mode note about a "session rotated by compaction".
- Failure midway: the findings do not describe what a failed compaction leaves behind in the transcript. The safeguard writes a degraded structured fallback summary rather than failing; overflow recovery reports a typed error. Trust: inferred.

#### Instruction files

- Injectable set is exactly `AGENTS.md, SOUL.md, IDENTITY.md, USER.md, BOOTSTRAP.md, MEMORY.md`, in that order, from the workspace, as "Project Context" in the cached prefix. Defaults: 20,000 characters per file, 60,000 total, `USER.md` 4,000. A cut file keeps 75% head and 25% tail. `AGENTS.md` is cut as 45% head, 15% tail and 35% of the budget as a "Policy digest" of lines matching words such as must, never, do not, security, secret, credential, test, commit, push, PR (and some CJK prohibition words), with a "[truncated, read AGENTS.md for full content]" marker; the pieces are shrunk tail, then head, then digest until it fits. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/workspace-bootstrap-policy.ts#L30-L37 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-helpers/bootstrap-limits.ts#L1-L3 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-helpers/bootstrap.ts#L87-L101 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/embedded-agent-helpers/bootstrap.ts#L207-L262 . Trust: checked in review (the Round 1 claim that `TOOLS.md` and `HEARTBEAT.md` are injected is refuted).
- Per session type: sub-agent sessions get only `AGENTS.md`; cron sessions get `AGENTS, SOUL, IDENTITY, USER`; `MEMORY.md` is dropped for sub-agent, cron, group and channel chats (privacy filter). Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/workspace.ts#L859-L866 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/workspace.ts#L896-L917 . Trust: checked in review.
- The input files do not describe ancestor-directory discovery or a trust gate for these files; they are workspace files of the agent. Trust: absence not confirmed.

#### Memory and session search

- What is stored: `MEMORY.md` and `memory/*.md` are ordinary workspace files; `memory/YYYY-MM-DD.md` receives flush notes; short-term recall entries feed dreaming. `MEMORY.md` has a 10,000-character budget (https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/memory-core/src/memory-budget.ts#L17-L17 ).
- Who writes: the model's `write`/`edit` tools in any full run; the flush turn (append-only to today's file); the dreaming job. Nothing asks a person. The only guard is the read-side privacy filter. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/workspace.ts#L896-L917 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/memory-core/src/short-term-promotion-apply.ts#L380-L400 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked).
- Search: `memory_search` is hybrid, vector 0.7 and keyword 0.3, 4x candidate multiplier, MMR re-ranking with lambda 0.7, temporal decay with a 30-day half-life; only `maxResults`, `minScore`, sources and extra paths come from config. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/agents/memory-search.ts#L145-L173 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/memory-core/src/memory/hybrid.ts#L52-L80 . Trust: read the definition (unchecked).
- Dreaming (background curator): a cron job, default `0 3 * * *`, scores short-term entries. A candidate needs at least 3 recalls, at least 3 distinct user queries (synthetic recall keys cannot count), and weighted score at least 0.75 (`0.24*frequency + 0.30*relevance + 0.15*diversity + 0.15*recency + 0.10*consolidation + 0.06*conceptual` plus a phase boost; recency half-life 14 days; snippets capped at 160 tokens). Candidates go to a consolidation sub-agent that returns add, merge or supersede operations; the result is validated, and a plan removing more than 25% of prior entries is rejected, in which case entries are appended. If `MEMORY.md` exceeds 10,000 characters the oldest generated sections are dropped, within the same 25% bound. Added entries are bounded only by the 10,000-character budget. Writes go through a locked path. Trace: https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/src/memory-host-sdk/dreaming.ts#L22-L42 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/memory-core/src/short-term-promotion.ts#L28-L40 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/memory-core/src/short-term-promotion.ts#L100-L160 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/memory-core/src/dreaming-consolidation.ts#L387-L460 , https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/8bac830dcb8ff99055ba71558dd369a989cc7881/extensions/memory-core/src/dreaming-consolidation.ts#L275-L295 . Trust: checked in review for thresholds and guard; the consolidation prompt was not read.
- Ledger and rollback: the findings describe none for `MEMORY.md` (only the 25% bound and an append-only fallback). Skill Workshop changes are versioned and undoable (manifest help text; archive job not traced).
- Session search: none found beyond memory search (not separately searched by a reviewer).

### Hermes Agent

Repository `NousResearch/hermes-agent`, pinned `95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02`, Python, read only. Sources: `hermes-agent.agents.md` (findings 3.1, 3.3 to 3.7, section 8 review: 21 of 45 findings checked, all supported or partly), `hermes-agent.sessions.md` (compaction in the session store) and `hermes-agent.safety.md` (threat-pattern scan). Not traced: the gateway 85% "hygiene" compaction, micro-compaction and `compression.prepare_ahead`, native/Codex compaction.

#### Prompt assembly and cache

1. `build_system_prompt_parts` returns three tiers. `stable` holds identity, guidance and the coding brief. `context` holds the caller's `system_message`, project context files and a worktree snapshot. `volatile` holds the skills index (first), memory blocks, plugin sections, profile line, timestamp and runtime environment. Worktree-dependent blocks sit after project context so a shared AGENTS.md can stay in the longest common prefix. The joined string is cached in `agent._cached_system_prompt`; `invalidate_system_prompt` clears it and reloads memory from disk, and compaction calls it. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/system_prompt.py#L751-L814, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/system_prompt.py#L817-L826, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/system_prompt.py#L829-L847. Trust: checked in review.
2. The git "workspace snapshot" is pinned per session and seeded from the persisted prompt, so a resumed session does not re-probe git and rewrite the prefix (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/system_prompt.py#L662-L707). A stored prompt is reused as the static part only if it literally starts with the rebuilt stable tier (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/system_prompt.py#L850-L876). Trust: unchecked.
3. Guidance text is data in code, gated on the tool being present and on model-name substring lists (`gpt`, `codex`, `gemini`, `gemma`, `grok`, `glm`, `qwen`, `deepseek`), each overridable by config. `SOUL.md` in the profile home replaces the default identity. Memory guidance is adapted when only USER.md is enabled. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/system_prompt.py#L563-L600, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_builder.py#L362-L376, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_builder.py#L195-L233. Trust: unchecked.
4. The skills index shows 60 characters of each description (`SKILL_PROMPT_DESC_LIMIT = 60`); `skill_manage` returns a warning when a new description would be cut (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/skill_utils.py#L846-L846, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_manager_tool.py#L432-L438). A successful skill write clears the in-process skills cache, but the live prompt is re-rendered only at the next compaction or session (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_manager_tool.py#L760-L763). Trust: checked in review (the 60-character limit).
5. Tool descriptions are rewritten on every `get_definitions()` pass from live config: `delegate_task` states the real `max_concurrent_children` and mentions nested delegation only when `max_spawn_depth >= 2`; `memory` builds its schema from the enabled stores (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/delegate_tool.py#L684-L706, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/delegate_tool.py#L751-L770, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/memory_tool.py#L442-L450). A code comment says this can change the `tools[]` bytes, so the background-review fork inherits the parent's tool list and restricts only at dispatch. Trust: unchecked.
6. Cache markers: four `cache_control` breakpoints by default (static system prefix, end of system prompt, last two non-system messages; without a static prefix one system plus last three). One-shot requests (no tools, at most one user turn: summaries, titles) get no marker. Route quirks are encoded (LiteLLM-style proxies reject part markers on `role: tool`; Alibaba-family providers get 5 minutes only; a measured one-hour allow-list). Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_caching.py#L1-L6, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_caching.py#L36-L49, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_caching.py#L117-L132. Trust: unchecked (reviewer did not check prompt caching).
7.
