Cache parity for forks: the background-review fork reuses the parent's cached system prompt and tool list byte-identical so its request hits the parent's cache (a code comment claims about 26% cost saving; vendor-stated, not measured here): https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/background_review.py#L1022-L1034. On a model fallback the identity line in the system prompt is rewritten, which misses the cache once: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/chat_completion_helpers.py#L2067-L2067. Trust: unchecked.

#### Compaction

**Trigger and size measurement.** `threshold_tokens = (context_length - max_tokens) * threshold_percent`. `threshold` defaults to 0.50; windows under 512K are floored at 0.75 (raise only). The result is floored at a minimum context length but capped at 85% of the usable window if the floor would swallow the headroom. An absolute `threshold_tokens` cap and per-model `model_thresholds` (longest substring key wins, optionally `provider:substr`) can lower it. `should_compress` is `prompt_tokens >= threshold_tokens` unless a cooldown or the "ineffective" breaker blocks it. The count used is provider-reported prompt usage when available and a rough estimate before the first response. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/hermes_cli/config_defaults.py#L563-L595, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L2751-L2755, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L2764-L2797, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L2026-L2050, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L2990-L3003. Trust: checked in review.

**Breaker ("check that it shrank", in part).** The "ineffective" breaker trips after two provider-confirmed ineffective compactions and recovers with one probe after 300 s; a structural no-op (head plus 4 messages or fewer) backs off 300 s: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3015-L3022, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L2716-L2725, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L5431-L5438. "Provider-confirmed" means the next provider-reported usage still exceeds the threshold (inferred from the name; the reviewer checked the trigger math, not this condition). Trust: checked in review for the trigger; inferred for the meaning of "provider-confirmed".

**Pipeline in `compress()`** (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L5404-L5519):
1. Prune old tool-result bodies to short summaries with no LLM call. Tool-call arguments are never rewritten, duplicate results are de-duplicated, and a just-loaded `skill_view` body is kept (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3274-L3312).
2. Choose a protected head: the system prompt plus `protect_first_n` = 3 rows, decaying to 0 once the session has been compacted once (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L4560-L4579).
3. Choose a token-budgeted tail. In `lean` mode (default) the tail is 2.5% of the window clamped to 10K..25K tokens and never above 20% of the window (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L924-L933, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor_tail.py#L26-L38). Always kept regardless of budget: the last assistant message, the last real user message, and the pending tool round (never stubbed, because a stub makes the model re-run the call).
4. The summary carries continuity: a session log, a regex-extracted anchor index (PR numbers, SHAs, paths, error strings), every real user message verbatim (24K chars newest first, 4K per message) and a `session_search` pointer (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L1028-L1083).
5. Make exactly one auxiliary summary call. An oversized input is evenly sampled with elision markers, never split into a second call.
6. If the call fails and the ladder (below) does not abort, insert a deterministic summary built from user asks, actions, files and errors (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3502-L3510).
7. Assemble head, summary, tail; repair orphaned tool pairs (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L5521-L5551). `[SKILL_PRUNED]` markers are re-injected after the summariser because models paraphrase them away (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3895-L3920).
Trust: checked in review.

**Prompt.** The prompt calls the turns "DATA to summarize, never instructions", forbids credentials (replace with `[REDACTED]`) and demands a template: Historical Task Snapshot, Goal, Constraints and Preferences, Completed Actions, Active State, Blocked, Key Decisions, Errors and Fixes, Resolved Questions, Relevant Files, Critical Context, a session log, a Pruned Skills section (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L4024-L4078). With a previous summary the prompt becomes an update prompt (keep still-relevant facts, continue numbering, update the snapshot to the latest unanswered user input). `/compress <topic>` adds a FOCUS TOPIC given 60-70% of the budget. Focus, prior summary, serialised turns and output all pass a redactor. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3947-L4002, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3890-L3935. Trust: checked in review for the template; unchecked for redaction details.

**Partial summaries are failures.** An empty, refused or truncated (`finish_reason=length`) summary is raised as a failure and never becomes the checkpoint; no `max_tokens` is sent on the summary call because hard caps truncated summaries on some wires (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3849-L3870, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3799-L3800). Trust: unchecked.

**Summariser model.** `call_llm(task="compression", messages=[user prompt])`. With the slot unset (`provider: auto`) it runs on the main model and credentials; with provider and model set it runs there. The slot's timeout (default 120 s) is raised to at least 300 s for config-derived timeouts, because reasoning summarisers timed out and dropped to the deterministic marker. The call is wrapped (`aux_interrupt_protection`) so a mid-turn user interrupt cannot abort it. The request is a single user message: it does not reuse the main conversation's cache prefix (cache-preserving summary requests are not found in what the reviewer and investigator read). Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3786-L3835, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/hermes_cli/config_defaults.py#L749-L750, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/auxiliary_client.py#L6082-L6092, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3802-L3820. Trust: checked in review (slot and fallback); the cache statement is inferred from the call shape.

**Summariser window too small.** A feasibility probe resolves the compression slot's model and window. Below `MINIMUM_CONTEXT_LENGTH` the session refuses to start (`ValueError`). Below the main model's compression threshold, the live threshold is lowered to the aux window and stored as a durable ceiling (`_aux_context_ceiling`) re-applied on every recomputation; the user gets a notice with two config fixes. If the aux model is the main model on the same route, the main window is reused. The probe re-runs after a model switch, a fallback activation or a changed compression route. With no usable aux provider, the probe warns "older messages will be cut without a summary" and the deterministic fallback is used. A probe failure other than the hard rejection is swallowed and retried at the first compaction. Reviewer note: the lowered trigger equals the whole summariser window, so it leaves no headroom for the prompt template and the output. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/conversation_compression.py#L2028-L2117, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/conversation_compression.py#L2091-L2102, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/conversation_compression.py#L1911-L1933, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L2739-L2748, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/conversation_compression_feasibility.py#L64-L127. Trust: checked in review.

**Failure ladder.** If a different summary model was in use, retry once on the main model immediately. Otherwise classify: "no provider" arms a 600 s cooldown; timeouts escalate 60/300/900 s; JSON-decode, streaming-closed and empty-content get 30 s; others 60 s. Terminal network, truncation, empty-content and auth failures make `compress()` abort and leave the session unchanged instead of replacing the middle with a placeholder; sustained overload stops being terminal after three strikes. Otherwise the deterministic fallback summary is inserted. Manual `/compress` passes `force=True` and clears the cooldown. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L4080-L4175, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L3761-L3784, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L800-L800, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/context_compressor.py#L5408-L5416. Trust: checked in review.

**Recovery from a provider context-length error.** Three shapes:
- HTTP 413: compress and score progress in serialised payload bytes, not tokens (the token estimator is blind to images and wedged sessions). If compression cannot shrink the request, strip retained image payloads and retry once more before failing (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/turn_overflow.py#L219-L275).
- "Prompt too long": adopt the provider-reported limit (persisted per model and endpoint) and compress. A guessed probe tier is never adopted, only a provider-reported number (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/turn_overflow.py#L315-L340, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/turn_overflow.py#L359-L451).
- "Output cap too large": clamp `max_tokens` to the provider's available tokens minus 64 and also compress; an unparsable output-cap error fails fast (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/turn_overflow.py#L278-L312).
A rejection on a local endpoint whose request is under half the known window is treated as transient and not compressed. Attempts are bounded by `compression.max_attempts` (default 3, cap 10). Trust: unchecked (the reviewer listed the provider-overflow ladder as not checked).

#### Pruning of old tool output

Two mechanisms. (1) At compaction, old tool-result bodies are projected to short summaries without an LLM (step 1 above). (2) At call time, a result over its threshold (default 100,000 chars; 50,000 for `mcp_` tools; `read_file` pinned to infinity so reading cannot be re-spilled) is written under `$HERMES_HOME/cache/spillover/` and replaced by a 1,500-char preview plus the path; an aggregate per-turn budget of 200,000 chars also applies; spill files are age-swept (24 h fallback). Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/tool_result_storage.py#L1-L8, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/budget_config.py#L8-L20. Trust: checked in review. There is no separate between-compaction pruning pass in what was read (micro-compaction exists by name and was not traced).

#### Record in the log and failure mid-way

`compression.in_place` is true by default: the live message list and system prompt are rewritten on the same session id, and old rows are soft-archived (`active=0, compacted=1`), still found by `session_search`. `False` selects the legacy rotation with a child session (`model_config._compressed_from`, parent ends with `end_reason='compression'`). The commit bumps `runtime_generation` so a stale worker is fenced. An aborted compression returns the input unchanged and does not split the session. Hermes' own config comment names a "session-rotation bug cluster" as the reason for in-place. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/hermes_cli/config_defaults.py#L667-L671, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/conversation_compression.py#L3958-L3980, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/hermes_state_mutation_compress.py#L12-L21, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/session_search_tool.py#L400-L416. Trust: checked in review.

#### Instruction files

Precedence: `.hermes.md`/`HERMES.md` (walk up to git root), then the `AGENTS.md` chain (git root down to cwd; per directory `AGENTS.override.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `agents.md`, first non-empty wins; identical content de-duplicated), then `CLAUDE.md` (cwd only), then `.cursorrules` plus `.cursor/rules/*.mdc`. The first type with any non-empty file wins; the rest are shadowed. Each file is capped at 20,000 chars, or without an explicit setting 6% of the model window in chars clamped to 20K..500K, keeping head 70% and tail 20%. A cwd that is only a fallback inside the Hermes install tree gets no project context. File reads have a 5 s timeout. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_builder.py#L1580-L1655, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_builder.py#L1720-L1749, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_builder.py#L1033-L1054, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_builder.py#L1658-L1665, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_builder.py#L42-L53. Trust: traced end to end (investigator); not in the reviewer's list.

Trust model: one regex library (`tools/threat_patterns.py`) with three cumulative scopes: `all` (classic "ignore previous instructions", hidden HTML comments or divs, curl/wget with secret variables), `context` (adds role hijack, C2 vocabulary, anti-forensics), `strict` (adds `authorized_keys`, `~/.ssh` writes, edits to AGENTS.md or config, hardcoded secrets). Input is NFKC-normalised, invisible and bidi codepoints are flagged, only the first 64 KiB is scanned. A repo context file (AGENTS.md, .cursorrules, .hermes.md) with any hit is replaced by `[BLOCKED: ...]`; the user's own `SOUL.md` is loaded with a warning. Output of `web_*`, `browser_*` and `mcp_*` tools is wrapped in `<untrusted_tool_result>` delimiters with forged delimiters defanged. `terminal` and `read_file` output is not in the untrusted set. One false positive loses a whole project AGENTS.md. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/threat_patterns.py#L16-L39, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/threat_patterns.py#L131-L145, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/prompt_builder.py#L84-L110, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/tool_dispatch_helpers.py#L484-L493. Trust: checked in review.

#### Memory and session search

**Store.** `MEMORY.md` (agent notes, 2,200 chars) and `USER.md` (user profile, 1,375 chars) in `<HERMES_HOME>/memories/`, per profile. Entries are joined by `\n§\n`; the limit counts the joined string. `add` is refused over the limit and returns the live entries so the model can consolidate; after three failed consolidations in one turn the result is terminal. Model actions: `add`, `patch`, `remove` (`replace` removed from the schema, replayable for staged writes). Every mutation takes an exclusive lock on a sibling `.lock`, re-reads from disk, and refuses to write if the file exists but cannot be read or if free text made one entry larger than the whole limit (a `.bak` is saved). Files over the limit at load are kept, not truncated. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/agent_init.py#L1194-L1202, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/memory_tool_store.py#L177-L221, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/memory_tool_store.py#L350-L395, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/memory_tool_store.py#L51-L62. Trust: checked in review.

**Prompt placement.** `load_from_disk` captures a per-target snapshot; `format_for_system_prompt` returns the snapshot, never live state, in the volatile tier. Entries matching threat patterns are replaced by `[BLOCKED: ...]` in the snapshot only; the live list keeps the raw text so the user can see and delete it. Writes are scanned with the strict scope and blocked. The snapshot refreshes when the prompt is rebuilt after compaction. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/memory_tool_store.py#L223-L255, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/memory_tool_store.py#L649-L652, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/system_prompt.py#L846-L847. Trust: unchecked as a whole finding; the refresh at invalidation is checked in review.

**Who writes and who approves.** `memory.write_approval` and `skills.write_approval` default to false (free writes). When true: a foreground memory write prompts inline; if nobody can answer (cron, kanban worker, api_server, webhook) or the prompt fails, it is staged as `pending/memory/<id>.json` for `/memory pending|approve|reject`; a background-review write is always staged; a denied inline prompt blocks. For replace/remove the matched entry is pinned at staging time so approval applies to what the person saw. The gate fails open if its module cannot be imported. A disk failure while staging loses the write (called the safe failure). Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/write_approval.py#L201-L216, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/write_approval.py#L219-L248, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/write_approval.py#L74-L89, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/memory_tool.py#L64-L82, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/memory_tool.py#L84-L90. Trust: checked in review.

**Unattended review cannot delete.** When the write origin is an unattended background review, `patch` and `remove` (single or in a batch) are staged, not applied; `add` still works; if staging fails the op is denied. `/refine` is marked attended and keeps the full set. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/memory_tool.py#L173-L219, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_provenance.py#L29-L44. Trust: checked in review.

**Background review.** Two counters trigger a forked review after a turn: memory every `memory.nudge_interval` user turns (default 10; 0 disables); skills after `skills.creation_nudge_interval` tool-calling iterations without a `skill_manage` call (code default 10; example config says 15). The fork is a second `AIAgent` on the main model, same session id, persistence disabled, at most 16 iterations and an input-token budget of 75% of the review model's window capped at 600K (120K if unknown). At dispatch only `skills` (plus `memory` when the memory trigger fired) and read-only `read_file`/`search_files` are allowed; terminal and file writes are denied and dangerous-command prompts auto-deny. It can be routed to another model through `auxiliary.background_review`, then it replays a compact digest. The enabled key fails open. A live user turn interrupts the fork. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/background_review.py#L173-L225, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/background_review.py#L979-L1061, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/background_review.py#L1116-L1131, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/background_review.py#L228-L250. Trust: checked in review.

**Skills as agent-written memory.** `skill_manage` (`create`, `patch`, `edit`, `delete`, `write_file`, `remove_file`): name 64 chars, description 1,024, SKILL.md 100,000 chars, supporting files 1 MiB; atomic writes with a per-skill lock; an opt-in post-write scan (`skills.guard_agent_created`, default off; the code comment says `terminal()` can run the same code ungated). With `skills.write_approval` on, every call from any origin is staged to `pending/skills/<id>.json`; approval replays the payload with the gate bypassed. Reviewer correction: replay has no base hash, mtime or version check, but a staged targeted `patch` still needs its `old_string` to match the current body, so a stale patch normally fails; full rewrites (`create`, `edit`) and a fuzzy match landing elsewhere are unguarded. Only skills the background review created are stamped `agent_created` and curator-managed. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_manager_tool.py#L108-L115, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_manager_tool.py#L672-L701, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_manager_tool.py#L716-L722, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_manager_tool.py#L549-L553, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_usage.py#L278-L318. Trust: corrected in review (staged-patch claim); checked in review otherwise.

**Curator.** No cron daemon: `maybe_run_curator` is called from CLI/TUI idle, web idle and a gateway poll. It runs if enabled (default on), not paused, `last_run_at` older than `interval_hours` (168) and idle at least `min_idle_hours` (2). The first observation only seeds `last_run_at`. An `O_EXCL` claim file under `skills/.locks/curator-run` makes one process win (stale after 3,600 s); it is released in `finally` right after the pass starts on a daemon thread, so it guards the start, not the duration; saving `last_run_at` first prevents a second start. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator.py#L31-L35, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator.py#L160-L179, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator.py#L1184-L1217, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator.py#L967-L974. Trust: checked in review.
- Phase 1, no model: managed skills go `active` to `stale` (14 days no activity) to `archived` (30 days), by moving the directory into `skills/.archive/` (restorable). Never touched: pinned, locked, protected built-ins, skills referenced by any cron job. Day counts below 1 fall back to the default (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator.py#L209-L263). Trust: checked in review.
- Phase 2, a model: off by default (`curator.consolidate` false). An `AIAgent` with only the `skills` toolset (no terminal), `max_iterations=9999`, nudges off, origin `background_review`; model from `auxiliary.curator` if set, else the main model. A delete must carry `absorbed_into=<umbrella>` naming an existing skill; a patch or `write_file` on an existing file is refused unless the fork `skill_view`ed it in this review. Terminal is withheld because a shell `mv` would leave no ledger entry (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator.py#L1103-L1142, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_manager_guards.py#L211-L221, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator.py#L1131-L1135). Trust: checked in review.
- Ledger: every skill mutation by `curator`, `agent` or `user` appends one JSON line to `skills/.curator_ledger.jsonl` with `before` and `after` manifests `{path, sha256}`; contents stored once under `.curator_backups/blobs/<sha256>`; unchanged files dropped; trimmed to 5 MB by default. `rollback_entry` checks that every blob exists, writes a `pre-rollback` entry capturing the current state, then restores `before` files and deletes files the mutation created; if the safety capture fails nothing changes. Entry paths must be under the profile home. Append is "telemetry, not a gate": a failed write is logged and the mutation proceeds, so the audit trail can have gaps. The sha256 is content addressing, not a hash chain; not tamper-evident; `terminal` writes bypass it. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_ledger.py#L1-L8, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_ledger.py#L298-L323, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_ledger.py#L557-L633. Trust: checked in review.
- Whole-tree snapshot (consolidation runs only): `skills/` tarred into `.curator_backups/<utc-id>/skills.tar.gz` with a manifest and a cron-job copy, pruned to `curator.backup.keep`. `rollback` first snapshots the current tree (aborts if that fails), extracts with path validation. A snapshot failure is only logged and the pass continues unprotected; the per-entry ledger is then the only undo. Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator_backup.py#L153-L192, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator_backup.py#L406-L481, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/agent/curator.py#L951-L958. Trust: checked in review.
- Lock: `hermes curator lock <skill>` sets `locked: true` in `skills/.usage.json`; `skill_manage`, `write_file`, `patch` and `terminal` (by parsing the command's write targets) refuse; the agent cannot run `unlock`. The module says it is a policy check, not a sandbox (https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_lock.py#L1-L13, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/skill_manager_guards.py#L224-L252). Trust: unchecked (lock parser not checked by the reviewer).

**Session search.** `session_search` uses SQLite FTS5 and no LLM (module docstring). Four shapes: discovery (`query`, de-duplicated by lineage, only the top result hydrated), scroll (`session_id` plus `around_message_id`), read (head and tail), browse. Subagent, kanban and tool-sourced sessions are hidden; cron sessions are demoted. Reads cap each message at 2,000 chars (a stored 74K-char tool result once inflated a request by about 40K tokens). Compaction-archived rows are still findable. The README still says "LLM summarization" (stale). Trace: https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/session_search_tool.py#L1-L9, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/session_search_tool.py#L354-L431, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/tools/session_search_tool.py#L34-L40, https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/95fc3e668fc1e13ad05d22a4f02fccc6a6d6ce02/README.md#L26-L26. Trust: checked in review.

### Oh My Pi

Repository `can1357/oh-my-pi` (`omp`), pinned `e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906`, TypeScript on Bun with Rust natives; read, not run. Sources: `oh-my-pi.agents.md` (compaction, system prompt, instruction files; review checked 17 of 35 findings, but the reviewer did NOT check snapcompact, compaction triggers, recovery from context-length errors, pruning, system-prompt assembly or instruction-file shadowing), `oh-my-pi.sessions.md` (compaction entry in the log, memory back ends; review checked 17 of 34, memory numbers checked, compaction replay and sharpshooter not checked). Compaction algorithms inside `packages/agent/src/compaction/` and the compaction prompts were not read in depth, and the semantics of the `handoff`, `shake` and `soft` methods are described only by their fall-through conditions.

#### Prompt assembly and cache

1. The system prompt is an ordered array of blocks from Handlebars templates. Block 0 is the bundled `system-prompt.md` (or a discovered template, or `custom-system-prompt.md` when a custom prompt is set), rendered with tools, skills, rules, environment, personality and feature flags. Then eval-prelude guidance. Then one trailing `<project-context>` block (context files, workspace tree, active repo, append text), so the static prefix caches across directories. Each preparation step has a deadline and falls back to a minimal default on timeout. A literal `SYSTEM.md` beats a template; project beats user. An invalid discovered template is ignored with a warning; an explicit one is fatal. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/system-prompt.ts#L1049-L1090, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/system-prompt.ts#L998-L1048, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/system-prompt.ts#L886-L912, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/system-prompt.ts#L261-L286. Trust: unchecked (the reviewer's "not checked" list names system-prompt assembly); Round 1 claim about `SYSTEM.md` and `APPEND_SYSTEM.md` confirmed by the investigator.
2. Sub-agent prompts: the child gets the normal default prompt blocks plus a sub-agent block (role body, shared `context`, plan, worktree notice, peer roster, yield protocol). Per-spawn text is placed after the static `<project-context>` block so sibling spawns share a cached prefix. The child is created with `excludeAgentsMd: true`: files named `AGENTS.md` are dropped by basename, other context files such as `CLAUDE.md` still pass. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/task/executor.ts#L4139-L4176, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/task/executor.ts#L4993-L4996, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/system-prompt.ts#L357-L360. Trust: checked in review (exclusion).
3. No finding in the input files says tool descriptions are assembled differently from the template data above (tools are fed to the template); where tool-description text lives was not separately traced. Dynamic state is in the trailing project-context block, not in per-step context messages or diffs.
4. Memory back ends append their text to the system prompt "on rebuild" through `buildDeveloperInstructions`, and `beforeAgentStartPrompt` stages recall for the first turn (see memory below). Trust: read the definitions.
5. Cache-aware pruning: the per-turn prune passes leave the warm provider prefix alone until the cache is cold (see pruning). TTSR (stream rules) is a related mechanism: injected rule names are session entries so fire-once state survives compaction, but the rule text is an ordinary hidden message that compaction treats like any old message (inferred: no step re-injects or pins it; Round 1 "injections survive compaction" is corrected to "suppression state survives"). Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/ttsr-coordinator.ts#L455-L462, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-context.ts#L368-L374. Trust: traced for state, inferred for text; not checked in review.

#### Compaction

**A chain of methods.** `compaction.methodOrder` defaults to `remote, snapcompact, handoff, shake, soft`. A method is skipped when unusable: `remote` needs a configured endpoint or a native route (OpenAI Responses compact, Anthropic compaction beta); `snapcompact` needs a model that accepts image input and is excluded for byte or media 413s; `handoff` is never used for the `overflow` reason. A remote method that just failed speculatively is skipped if a later method can run. An empty or all-unusable order returns "none", or blocks automatic continuation on a threshold pass after a fallback. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/compaction-methods.ts#L11-L51, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L203-L217, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L5237-L5293. Trust: checked in review.

**Fall-through and "did it shrink" gate.** Every failure path calls `runAutoCompaction` again with `methodIndex + 1`: a shake that reclaims too little; a handoff that returns no document; any snapcompact blocker (unrenderable glyphs, no frame budget, over the per-request image byte budget, no reduction, still over the window, or no headroom under the trigger while a later summarising method exists); a summary that would not reduce context; any thrown error before commit. Abort ends the chain. If snapcompact leaves no headroom but no later summariser is usable, it keeps the archive with a warning. A speculative background summary, when armed and still valid, is spliced in instead of a blocking call (not for snapcompact). Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L5309-L5326, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L5636-L5655, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L5677-L5732, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L5844-L5861, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L6116-L6169, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L6201-L6242. Trust: traced end to end by the investigator; not in the reviewer's checked set.

**Trigger and numbers.** Compact when context tokens exceed the threshold. The threshold is `thresholdTokens` if set, else a percentage, else window minus reserve; the reserve is max(15% of window, `reserveTokens` default 16,384), with a proportional fallback for small windows. Summaries are capped at 16,384 tokens. A 0.8 hysteresis band defines "pressure resolved". Mid-turn checks and async speculation are on; idle compaction is off (200,000 tokens and 300 s when on). Keep-recent 20,000 tokens (Round 1 claim confirmed). Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/agent/src/compaction/compaction.ts#L193-L235, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/agent/src/compaction/compaction.ts#L324-L353, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/agent/src/compaction/compaction.ts#L394-L407, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/context-settings.ts#L130-L156, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/context-settings.ts#L282-L313. Trust: traced end to end; the reserve and keep-recent numbers were confirmed in the Round 1 table (review checked the method order only).

**Size measurement.** The trigger's token count is floored by a local estimate of the stored conversation, so on-wire compression (provider-side request shrinking) cannot hide growth. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/agent/src/compaction/compaction.ts#L355-L381. The source of the base count (provider-reported usage versus estimate) is not stated in the findings. Trust: traced end to end, not checked in review.

**Snapcompact: no LLM call.** Discarded messages are serialised (tool results capped at 2,000 chars, argument values at 500, a call at 2,000; 60% head and 40% tail), normalised, folded with any previous archive's source text, laid out into at most 80 frames and rendered natively to PNG images of pixel-font text, with a per-provider shape. The summary message is a template that explains how to read the frames plus a FILES list; the frames are stored in the compaction entry's `preserveData` and reattached on every context rebuild. Re-compaction re-renders the old archive's text rather than stacking images; an archive without stored text loses its frames and counts as truncated; a reader that drops reasoning gets a re-rendered "without thinking" view, falling back to text without frames if that fails. The model only reads the images; rendering is local (Round 1 said "by a vision model": corrected). It is lossy (tool output is cut to 2k before rendering). Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/snapcompact/src/snapcompact.ts#L807-L819, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/snapcompact/src/snapcompact.ts#L2494-L2568, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/snapcompact/src/snapcompact.ts#L2570-L2655, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/snapcompact/src/prompts/snapcompact-summary.md#L1-L20, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/crates/pi-natives/src/snapcompact.rs#L1067-L1100, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L5126-L5155. Trust: traced end to end; not checked in review.

**Summariser model.** Candidates in order: the model's configured `compactionModel`, the current model, each chat role's resolved model, then the single largest-context available model. Candidates without an API key are skipped; an auth failure moves on; a provider-native compaction error moves on only within the same provider, otherwise it is rethrown; any other error is thrown and the method chain falls through. Because the last candidate is the largest-context model, a too-small summariser window is handled by selection, not by a stated window check; no explicit window check was reported. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L3708-L3752, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L3767-L3852, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/role-models.ts#L64-L67. Trust: traced end to end; the "no explicit window check" part is inferred from the finding text.

**Recovery from a context-length error.** On overflow evidence from the same model the failed assistant turn is removed from active context. If `contextPromotion.enabled` (default false) and the model has a `contextPromotionTarget`, the session switches model and retries without compacting. Otherwise recovery compaction runs with reason `overflow` (handoff excluded; snapcompact also excluded for byte or media 413s). Overflow is not a transport retry case. There is no deterministic non-LLM fallback in the findings beyond the later methods in the chain (`shake`, `soft`), whose mechanics were not described. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L3223-L3246, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/context-settings.ts#L76-L87, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/turn-recovery.ts#L1584-L1587. Trust: traced end to end; not checked in review.

**Prompt, verbatim retention, cache-preserving requests.** Not read: the compaction prompts and the algorithm in `prepareCompaction` are listed as not read in depth. Only the keep-recent budget (20,000 tokens) and the summary cap (16,384 tokens) are known. Whether the summary request reuses the main conversation's cache prefix is not established.

#### Pruning of old tool output

Before any summarising method, `supersedeReads` (default on) prunes older read results of a file that was read again, and `dropUseless` (default on) prunes results flagged useless once consumed. Both are cache-aware (they leave the warm provider prefix alone until the cache is cold) and protect plan and skill reads. The pruned branch is persisted. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/context-settings.ts#L359-L382, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-maintenance.ts#L817-L852, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/plan-mode/plan-protection.ts#L16-L32. Trust: read the definition, not checked in review. Persisted strings are also cut at 500,000 characters with a notice and image base64 over 1 KiB is moved to a content-addressed blob store (sessions file; not checked in review).

#### Record in the log

Compaction never deletes entries. A `compaction` entry on the session path holds the summary, `firstKeptEntryId`, `tokensBefore`, `method` and optional provider-native replay payloads in `preserveData`. When building model context, the latest compaction on the path (or, given a model, the newest one that model can replay) emits the summary first, then kept messages from `firstKeptEntryId` up to the compaction, then everything after. Old entries stay on disk; the display transcript can show them inline. `reset_boundary` (written by `/clear`) is a durable marker; the later of boundary or compaction wins. `firstKeptEntryId` can be empty for provider-native snapshots, handled through `providerReplayThroughEntryId`. Model-dependent replay means switching model can re-expand history behind a compaction the new model cannot read. The log is a tree (entries with `id` and `parentId`), so a compaction is an entry on one branch. A failure before commit throws and the chain falls through; nothing is committed until a method succeeds (the reviewer did not check this ordering beyond the thrown-error path). Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-entries.ts#L163-L187, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-context.ts#L253-L268, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-context.ts#L392-L397, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-context.ts#L538-L668, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-context.ts#L518-L537, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/session/session-context.ts#L636-L641. Trust: traced end to end for the fold; "compaction replay" listed as not checked by the reviewer; emission after line 668 not read.

#### Instruction files

Context-file providers register with priorities: native 100, Claude `.claude/` 80, `.agent(s)` 70, Codex 70, Gemini 60, OpenCode 55, GitHub 30, standalone `AGENTS.md` 10, standalone `CLAUDE.md` 10. The capability key is "user" or `project:<depth>`, so per depth one file wins; configured extras are additive. Files are sorted farthest first, and a file is dropped if a closer one contains all its paragraphs. `@path` imports are expanded. Walk-up stops at the repo root or home. `AGENTS.md` and standalone `CLAUDE.md` tie at 10, so a repo with both at the root loads only one; empty files do not claim the slot. The user-level `~/.claude` tree is opt-in. No size budget for instruction files is reported in the findings. Trust: the safety file's heading says there is no project trust gate (`isProjectTrusted()` always returns true); I did not read that finding's body or its review verdict, so treat it as unchecked here. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/capability/context-file.ts#L32-L46, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/discovery/claude.ts#L42-L45, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/discovery/agents-md.ts#L24-L29, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/discovery/claude-md.ts#L24-L30, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/discovery/helpers.ts#L723-L763, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/system-prompt.ts#L336-L401, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/discovery/claude.ts#L47-L69. Trust: traced end to end; instruction-file shadowing is in the reviewer's "not checked" list.

Foreign MCP configs from the Claude provider are read with `${VAR}` expansion into every string (commands, args, env, URL, headers) at discovery; project entries load first so a project `enabled: false` wins; `mcp.enableProjectConfig` defaults to true. A cloned repo's MCP file could put `${SOME_API_KEY}` in a header (inferred risk). Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/discovery/claude.ts#L86-L153, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/discovery/helpers.ts#L573-L588. Trust: checked in review (expansion) / inferred (the risk).

#### Memory and session search

**One interface, five mutually exclusive back ends, default off.** `memory.backend` is `off` (default), `local`, `hindsight`, `mnemopi` or `sharpshooter`; `resolveMemoryBackend` returns exactly one. RPC and ACP hosts start with memory off. The `MemoryBackend` interface has `start` (must be non-throwing), `buildDeveloperInstructions` (markdown appended to the system prompt on rebuild), `clear`, `enqueue`, optional `status`, `search`, `save`, `stats`, `diagnose`, `queuePreview`, `beforeAgentStartPrompt` (staged recall for the first turn with a commit step, so a recall the model never saw is not recorded), `preCompactionContext` (text added to the compaction prompt) and `applySettings`. A failed recall returns `undefined`, not an empty success, so it is not cached as empty. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/memory-backend/types.ts#L16-L16, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/memory-backend/types.ts#L100-L195, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/memory-backend/resolve.ts#L14-L26, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/memory-backend/settings.ts#L11-L21, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/memory-backend/types.ts#L134-L139. Trust: read the definitions (not checked in review).

**Hindsight and Mnemopi.** Hindsight is a hand-written fetch client for a Hindsight server (default `http://localhost:8888`, optional bearer token); it retains the conversation every 3 turns by default (Mnemopi every 4). Retain events go to a file outbox, replayed in batches of 16, quarantined after 7 days undelivered, quarantine kept 30 days, at most 1000 undelivered events per destination (writes fail rather than evict). Mnemopi is a local SQLite engine (working and episodic memory, optional local ONNX embeddings or an OpenAI-compatible endpoint; LLM use host-backed or remote). The local, Hindsight, Mnemopi and Sharpshooter write paths all call `redactMemorySecrets`, which removes provider token prefixes and credential-shaped runs after keywords like `secret`, `token`, `key`; redaction is pattern-based, so unknown secret formats pass. Writes are automatic (model extraction or a turn counter); no human approval step exists in the interface, and no rollback or ledger was reported. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/hindsight/client.ts#L1-L47, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/hindsight/settings.ts#L17-L20, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/hindsight/settings.ts#L173-L178, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/mnemopi/settings.ts#L282-L282, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/hindsight/outbox.ts#L9-L16, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/memory-backend/redact.ts#L1-L25. Trust: checked in review (numbers); redaction call sites not re-read by the reviewer.

**Sharpshooter.** Per user prompt, an extractor model emits deltas (architecture, product, style, constraint, rejected approach, correction) into a per-session queue; every 5 minutes a consolidation pass applies queued deltas per project into three markdown files (`architecture.md`, `product.md`, `style.md`) under `<agentDir>/memories/sharpshooter/<project bank>/`. A delta's evidence must be an exact substring of the user's prompt (enforced by the host), and consolidation admits a decision only when its lineage shows friction (user corrected, regression, subtle rule). Consolidation is single-writer through a lock file. Consolidation prompt not read. Trace: https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/sharpshooter/types.ts#L1-L60, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/sharpshooter/paths.ts#L1-L52, https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/blob/e325ffc73ab86e03bdd20a91b77d8e2b3964c906/packages/coding-agent/src/sharpshooter/extract.ts#L1-L45. Trust: read the definitions; unchecked.

**Local distillation, `learn`/`manage_skill` approval and session search.** Not traced (entry points `packages/coding-agent/src/memories` and `autolearn`). No session-search finding exists for Oh My Pi in the input files; the sessions file covers the entry tree and storage, not text search.

### Pi

Repository `earendil-works/pi`, pinned `91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af`. Sources: `pi.agents.md` (findings 1.1 to 1.4, 2.1 to 2.3, 4.1 to 4.6, 5.2, 10.1), `pi.sessions.md` (session storage, compaction entries), `pi.safety.md` (trust gate). Pi is a TypeScript monorepo; the loop is `packages/agent`, the harness is `packages/coding-agent`.

#### Prompt assembly and cache

- The system prompt is built as ordered named sections by `buildSystemPromptSections`: `preamble` (126 characters, one sentence), `tools` (one line per active tool that has a snippet), `rules` (deduplicated bullets), `docs` (paths to Pi's own docs), then if present `addendum` (APPEND_SYSTEM text), `project_context` (instruction files), `skills`, `cwd`. Every section except `preamble` is wrapped in `<name>...</name>`. There is no date, OS or git status, only `cwd`. About 2,000 characters for the four default tools (an estimate, not tokenizer-measured). Trust: checked in review (the preamble length was corrected from 133 to 126 characters; the 2,000-character total was not re-measured). https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/system-prompt.ts#L152-L169 and https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/system-prompt.ts#L171-L182
- Tool text lives next to the tool. Each built-in tool exports `{ snippet, guidelines }` used for the prompt, plus a separate long `description` for the provider tool schema. Tools without a `promptSnippet` are not listed in the prompt. When only bash is active, a rule "Use bash for file operations like ls, rg, find" is added. Trust: unchecked (finding 1.3 was not reviewed). https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/tools/edit.ts#L40-L46 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/system-prompt.ts#L108-L116
- The prompt and the tool list are transcript state, patched by diff. Each turn `_preparePromptAndToolLoadout` rebuilds the desired sections, diffs them against the sections replayed from the transcript (`diffSystemPromptSections`), and if anything changed appends a `system` message holding only the changed sections (null removes a section). Tool additions and removals are `toolsAdded` / `toolsRemoved` on system messages. Providers that accept mid-conversation system messages get each in place, rendered as `Updated system prompt section "name": ...`; other providers rebuild the head from the replayed state. The loop declares tool changes before each request. Result: the cached prefix is never rewritten; a change is a new entry at the end. Trust: checked in review. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/ai/src/types.ts#L559-L585 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/system-prompt.ts#L211-L228 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L1828-L1840 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L959-L992
- An extension can force the exact full prompt (`forceSystemPrompt`); the forced text is projected onto the request only, the transcript keeps the structured sections. Compaction keeps a snapshot: `buildContextEntries` drops older system messages from the kept range and the compaction entry carries their replay. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L1877-L1892 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L499-L509
- `SYSTEM.md` (project `.pi/` only if the project is trusted, else the agent dir) replaces the prompt: `tools`, `rules` and `docs` are dropped, but `addendum`, `project_context`, `skills`, `cwd` remain. `APPEND_SYSTEM.md` files are joined into `addendum`. Project and global are not merged; the first existing wins. If the `--system-prompt` path does not exist or cannot be read, the argument itself becomes the prompt text (a path typo becomes the system prompt). Trust: unchecked for the footgun; Round 1 claim confirmed. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/resource-loader.ts#L1245-L1271 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/resource-loader.ts#L195-L210

#### Compaction

Trust for the whole section: checked in review (findings 4.1 to 4.4 were reviewed; 4.5 was not).

1. **Trigger.** `shouldCompact` is `contextTokens > contextWindow - reserveTokens`; defaults `reserveTokens` 16,384, `keepRecentTokens` 20,000, enabled. Settings resolve per model (`modelOverrides`), then global, then default. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L126-L130 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L267-L270
2. **Size measurement.** `contextTokens` is the last valid assistant message's provider `usage.totalTokens` (or input + output + cacheRead + cacheWrite) plus a chars/4 estimate of the messages after it (an image counts as 4,800 characters). Usage from before the latest compaction is ignored (it reflects the old, larger context; otherwise a fresh compaction would immediately re-trigger). After errors or zero-usage responses it falls back to the pure estimate. A response from a different model than the current one does not trigger overflow handling. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L196-L224 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L298-L349 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L3122-L3130
3. **When it is checked.** (a) after each tool batch, before the next assistant request (`prepareNextTurn`, skipped for virtual models, which are checked after routing); (b) before a new run starts (`_prepareRun`, which also catches an aborted last response); (c) after the run ends (`_handlePostAgentRun`, `_checkCompaction`). A prompt submitted during compaction throws "Cannot submit a prompt while compaction is in progress"; queued steering is delivered afterwards. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L827-L846 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L1996-L2002 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L2078-L2080 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L3204-L3239
4. **Cut point.** `prepareCompaction` finds the previous compaction (its summary becomes `previousSummary`) and `findProjectedCutPoint` walks newest to oldest accumulating estimated tokens until `keepRecentTokens` is reached, then picks the closest valid cut point at or after that entry. Valid cut points are user, assistant, bash-execution, custom, branch-summary and compaction-summary messages; tool results are never cut points. If the cut lands mid-turn, `isSplitTurn` is set and the early part of that turn is summarised separately. A single recent tool result bigger than 20,000 tokens is kept in full, so the kept tail can exceed `keepRecentTokens` and, with a small window, still be over the threshold. If there is nothing to summarise, the manual command reports "Nothing to compact (session too small)" or "Already compacted". https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L789-L857 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L351-L379 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L859-L923
5. **Summariser model.** The session's current model and thinking level (a virtual model is routed first). There is no cheaper "compaction model" setting (a search of the settings files found none; the reviewer did not separately confirm this absence). The `reserveTokens` value is only the trigger margin: the summary request ignores it (`_reserveTokens` is an unused parameter; summaries use the model's output limit). https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L2878-L2901 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L644-L660
6. **Prompt.** The conversation is serialised to plain text (`[User]:`, `[Assistant]:`, `[Assistant tool calls]: name(k=v, ...)`, `[Tool result]:`) inside `<conversation>` tags, with a system prompt "You are a context summarization assistant... Do NOT continue the conversation". The instruction template has sections Goal, Constraints & Preferences, Progress (Done / In Progress / Blocked), Key Decisions, Next Steps, Critical Context, and "Preserve exact file paths, function names, and error messages". With a previous summary an UPDATE prompt is used with `<previous-summary>` and rules to preserve, add and move items (iterative summary). Tool results are cut to 2,000 characters, thinking blocks are omitted, tool-call arguments are kept in full. `/compact <text>` appends "Additional focus: <text>". For a split turn two summaries are made (history, plus a "Turn Prefix" prompt) and merged under a `**Turn Context (split turn):**` separator. `<read-files>` and `<modified-files>` lists are appended, accumulated from the previous compaction's details and the summarised tool calls. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L507-L579 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L929-L942 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/utils.ts#L94-L156 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L980-L1056
7. **Cache.** Summary calls use `cacheRetention: "none"` and a fresh routing id. This is the opposite of a cache-preserving summary request: the summary call re-sends and re-bills the full history uncached, and the serialised text form means it could not share the main loop's prefix anyway. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L618-L638 (the second sentence is my inference from the serialisation and the cache setting)
8. **What is kept.** `[compaction summary] + entries from firstKeptEntryId + everything after`; the model sees the summary as "The conversation history before this point was compacted into the following summary: ...". Nothing is deleted from the session file. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L476-L512 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/messages.ts#L11-L17
9. **Failure rules.** A summary response with `stopReason` `error` or `length`, or one containing a tool call, is a failure and is not persisted. Transient errors are retried with the agent retry policy (3 tries, 2,000 ms base doubling, 60,000 ms cap, no jitter); the summary call runs inside `retryAssistantCall` and emits `summarization_retry_*` events. A hook `session_before_compact` may cancel or supply the summary (summary then marked `fromHook`). https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/compaction.ts#L585-L593 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/ai/src/utils/retry.ts#L194-L262 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L3277-L3300
10. **No staged fallback, no shrink check, no input guard.** There is no truncate-and-retry, no "drop oldest and summarise again", and no check that the result is smaller. Nothing pre-checks that the messages to summarise fit the model's window: the author's own reading is that an oversized summary request returns an overflow error, which is excluded from retry, so `compact` fails; this was not traced to a test (see Not settled). The failure is emitted as `compaction_end` with an error plus a `session_compact_failed` event. Trust: absence of fallback checked in review (finding 4.3 supported); the overflow-on-summary consequence inferred.
11. **Overflow recovery (one compact-and-retry).** After a run, `_checkCompaction` classifies the last assistant message. A context-overflow error (a regex table over provider error text with a non-overflow exclusion list such as rate limits, a 413 rule, "silent overflow" where input tokens exceed the window, and a "length stop with zero output filling the window" rule) or a `length` stop below the desired output limit selects recovery. Before compacting it appends `context_edit` entries (replacement null) that hide the failed assistant message and its tool results from the model projection while the raw log keeps them (`_omitRecoveryAttempt`). It then compacts with reason `overflow` and calls `agent.continue()` so the model re-answers the last input. `_overflowRecoveryAttempted` allows exactly one attempt per run, reset on the next successful assistant message; a second failure emits "Context overflow recovery failed after one compact-and-retry attempt. Try reducing context or switching to a larger-context model." A successful response that nevertheless exceeded the window compacts without retrying. If recovery compaction fails or is cancelled, the omission edits stay, no compaction entry is appended and no retry is scheduled. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/ai/src/utils/overflow.ts#L39-L86 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/ai/src/utils/overflow.ts#L144-L188 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L3132-L3202 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L1309-L1337
12. **Truncated tool calls are never run.** A `length` stop with tool calls turns each call into an error result "...hit the output token limit, so its arguments may be truncated. Re-issue the tool call", because partial JSON may still parse and validate. Trust: checked in review as part of 4.4. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/agent/src/agent-loop.ts#L272-L281
13. **Branch summaries** (on `/tree` navigation with "summarize"): collect from the old leaf back to the common ancestor, fill a budget of (window minus `branchSummary.reserveTokens` 16,384) newest to oldest with a 90% allowance for summaries, one LLM call with a shorter template (no Critical Context), stored as a `branch_summary` entry attached at the navigation target. Tool results are skipped entirely (compaction truncates them to 2,000 characters). Failure throws and the navigation does not happen. Trust: unchecked (4.5 not reviewed). https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/compaction/branch-summarization.ts#L203-L261 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L4271-L4280

#### Pruning

- Nothing trims or replaces old tool results between compactions: the model sees every tool result in full until a compaction summarises it. The only size control is at production: `truncate.ts` sets 2,000 lines and 50 KB; `read` (head), `ls`, `find`, `grep` (plus 500 characters per match line) and `bash` (tail) apply it themselves. Bash streams into an `OutputAccumulator` that keeps a bounded tail and spills the full output to a temp file, then tells the model the path. The loop and the extension wrapper apply no generic cap, so an extension tool returning 10 MB goes straight into context. Trust: 4.6 and 10.1 unchecked, except the two constants, which a reviewer confirmed. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L519-L574 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/tools/truncate.ts#L9-L11 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/tools/output-accumulator.ts#L80-L110
- The append-only `context_edit` entry (replace or omit an earlier entry in the projection) exists but only the overflow-recovery path and extension hooks create it; no interactive component does (a grep over `src/modes` found only tree-selector rendering). https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L1022-L1025

#### Log record

- A session is a JSONL file with a tree: each entry has `id` and `parentId`; `leafId` is the last entry in file order; branching changes only the in-memory leaf. `CompactionEntry` holds `summary`, `firstKeptEntryId`, `tokensBefore`, optional `details`, `usage`, `fromHook` and a `systemMessage` snapshot (the full system prompt and tool state at that point). `appendCompaction` writes it as a child of the leaf, after summarising succeeded. Old entries are never removed; they are simply not on the compacted path. The model context is a pure function of (entries, leaf): `buildSessionPath`, then `buildContextEntries` (latest compaction, kept range with system messages dropped, then later entries), then `buildSessionProjection` applying `context_edit`. A failed compaction therefore leaves the log untouched except for the omission edits of overflow recovery. Trust: checked in review. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L91-L116 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L1272-L1298 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/session-manager.ts#L390-L416 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/agent-session.ts#L3330-L3335
- The projection is recomputed from the whole entry list on every call (linear in session size).

#### Instruction files

- Per directory, `loadContextFileFromDir` tries `AGENTS.override.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `AGENTS.MD`, `CLAUDE.md`, `CLAUDE.MD` in that order and takes the first regular file. `loadProjectContextFiles` loads the agent-dir file first, then walks from cwd up to the filesystem root, so the final order is global, outermost ancestor, ..., cwd. Duplicates by path are skipped; a linked worktree's own file shadows the main repo's copy. Each file is injected as `<project_instructions path="...">` inside `project_context`. There is no byte or token cap and no repository-boundary stop. Files in subdirectories are not loaded when the model enters them. An unreadable file logs a warning and the next candidate is tried. Trust: checked in review. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/resource-loader.ts#L212-L231 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/resource-loader.ts#L260-L302 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/resource-loader.ts#L242-L258 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/system-prompt.ts#L79-L86
- Trust: context files load regardless of project trust (the call does not consult `isProjectTrusted`). The trust gate covers `.pi/{settings.json, mcp.json, extensions, skills, prompts, themes, SYSTEM.md, APPEND_SYSTEM.md}` and `.agents/skills`, not context files. A hostile repository's AGENTS.md therefore steers the model, and with default tools the model can then write `.pi/extensions/x.ts`. Trust is keyed by path, not content. Trust: gate behaviour confirmed in the safety file's Round 1 table; the attack chain is the author's reasoning. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/resource-loader.ts#L679-L685
- Skills: only name, description and absolute path go in the prompt (`<available_skills>`); the model reads the body with `read`. Name max 64, description max 1,024 characters; there is no total catalogue budget. Project `.pi/skills` and ancestor `.agents/skills` (to the git root) are trust-gated and come before user directories; first name wins, later ones give a `collision` diagnostic. Trust: unchecked. https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/skills.ts#L358-L388 , https://github.com/earendil-works/pi/blob/91d9719b4123a83816249d7a7917c75126bb55af/packages/coding-agent/src/core/skills.ts#L426-L455

#### Memory and session search

Nothing found. Searched `pi.agents.md`, `pi.sessions.md` and `pi.safety.md` for memory, session search and recall; the only hits were unrelated (QuickJS memory limits, in-memory stores). The input files do not claim absence was confirmed by a reviewer. Session resume (`-c`, `--session`, `-r` picker) exists but is not a search over content as far as the files say.

### DeepSeek harness

Repository `deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness`, pinned `fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159`. TypeScript on Node; everything is a Cordis plugin mounted from a YAML "profile". Sources: `deepseek-harness.agents.md` (system prompt, compaction, instruction files, memory), `deepseek-harness.sessions.md` (search index). Compaction is an abstract service `ctx.compaction` with the provider in `packages/compaction/compaction-basic`; the surface (the replayable message list) is rewritten with `replace` ops and no log event is deleted.

#### Prompt assembly and cache

- The system prompt is ordered named sections. `SystemPrompt` keeps sections with a numeric `order` from a central table (`SECTION_ORDERS`: identity -1000, persona prefix 0, plan 500, team 600, per-tool sections 1000 to 3000, MCP 3100, persona suffix 10200), sorted by order then by name. A per-agent registration with the same name shadows the global one; a duplicate in one layer throws. `{{variable}}` is strict (unknown variable throws). A "complete" section replaces all others (used by the `minimal` persona); more than one complete section throws. Tool sections return an empty string when their tool is not visible in the scope, so the prompt follows the tool set. The identity line is the only harness-owned text in the base. Plan and team text appears only when the logged session state is active; plan text is deployment config (YAML), in the base bundle for headless/ACP/SDK profiles and in the preset files for the web profile.
