Trust: checked in review (plan and team finding partly: citation widened). https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/core/system-prompt/src/index.ts#L136-L172 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/core/system-prompt/src/index.ts#L620-L697 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/fs/tool-fs/src/edit.ts#L76-L83 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/plan/plan-mode/src/index.ts#L197-L205 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/bundle/web-app/presets/standard.patch.yml#L54-L68
- Dynamic facts stay out of the system prompt. A second table `CONTEXT_ORDERS` (working directory, sandbox policy, approval policy, sub-agent delegation, process-specific paths) is rendered into one `runtime-context` user message each step. The snapshot is refreshed every step but a new message is appended to the log only when the rendered text differs from the retained snapshot; a "cleared" notice is emitted when none apply. Whether older snapshots are dropped from the surface was not traced. The system prompt itself is re-committed only when its rendered text, the surface or the tool set changes, and, depending on route capability, as a later `system` message in history rather than a replacement. Tool-set changes are detected against the logged request header. A plugin may suppress runtime context (the `minimal` preset does); required contexts survive suppression. Trust: corrected in review (appended only when text changes). https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/core/system-prompt/src/index.ts#L177-L184 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/core/agent-loop/src/agent.ts#L441-L478 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/core/agent-loop/src/runtime-context.ts#L1-L21 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/core/agent-loop/src/runtime-context.ts#L148-L169 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/core/agent-loop/src/agent.ts#L319-L324

#### Compaction

1. **Trigger.** A listener on `agent/pre-step` calls `compactIfNeeded(agent, 'pressure')` before every step. It prices the latest logged request header with the token meter, resolves the model's context window via `llm.resolveModelInfo`, and compacts when `totalTokens >= thresholdTokens`, with `threshold = floor(min(contextWindow * thresholdRatio, contextWindow - reservedCompletion - headroomTokens))`. Defaults: `thresholdRatio` 0.8, `headroomTokens` 65,536 (also the summary `maxTokens`), `retainRatio` 0.16 of the message budget, 1 extra compaction retry, 1 overflow retry, `auto` true. Per-model overrides come from `modelPolicies`. A model with no known context window throws a config error that is warned once per model and the turn continues uncompacted. Trust: checked in review. https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/config.ts#L20-L23 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/config.ts#L68-L110 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/config.ts#L191-L205 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/index.ts#L157-L175 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/index.ts#L316-L371
2. **Size measurement.** The token meter prices the logged request header (route-specific token counts). Retention and the shrink check use route-specific counts; shadow-price accounting uses a fixed heuristic (region.ts lines 376 to 381). The input files do not say whether the meter uses provider-reported usage. Trust: partly unchecked.
3. **Failure of the pre-step compaction.** If still above threshold after `compactionRetries + 1` attempts it throws; the pre-step listener swallows that, logs "step compaction failed ...; continuing the turn", and the request is sent anyway and may then hit a context-length error. Trust: checked in review.
4. **Summariser model: the conversation's own, over the exact prefix (cache-preserving).** The summariser replays the conversation's own system prompt, tool schemas and the shadowed messages, then appends the compaction instruction as the FINAL user message, not a separate summariser system prompt, so the provider prefix cache is reused. Target model: configured `summarizationProvider/Model`, else the last logged request route, else the agent's creation options. The full tool schemas are still passed while the model is told not to call tools; tool calls in the reply are dropped (text blocks only). Output must be text only (images rejected), non-empty, and not truncated at `maxTokens` (a `max-tokens` finish is an error). A prior `<compacted-summary>` block is to be merged, not copied. Trust: traced end to end; the summariser-model finding was not in the reviewed set ("Not checked: ... summariser model choice, what-is-kept details, recovery and model-switch details"). https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/summarizer.ts#L32-L67 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/summarizer.ts#L120-L181 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/summarizer.ts#L196-L221 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/region.ts#L544-L563
5. **Prompt.** Eight fixed Markdown sections, "do not call any tool" (text in summarizer.ts lines 32 to 67; the section names are not listed in the input).
6. **What is kept verbatim.** `selectCompactableRange` walks the surface from the newest node backwards accumulating route-priced tokens until `retainTokens` (default 16% of the message budget) is reached; that tail stays verbatim. The cut then moves earlier until it does not split a tool-call/result pair. The system head (surface node 0) is excluded. Everything from the first non-system node to the cut is replaced by ONE user message framed with a preamble and `<compacted-summary>...</compacted-summary>`. Nothing else (file contents, skills, instruction files) is re-injected after compaction in this path. A single oversized retained unit cannot be repaired by surface compaction (doc comment). Trust: unchecked (what-is-kept details not reviewed). https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/region.ts#L117-L155 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/summarizer.ts#L69-L71 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/summarizer.ts#L188-L194
7. **Check that it actually shrank.** The summary is rejected unless its framed token estimate is strictly smaller than the span's route-priced size (region.ts lines 417 to 422). Automatic compaction also demands the whole surface be unchanged during summarisation (stability check); manual compaction only the selected span. Trust: unchecked beyond the finding being traced end to end. https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/region.ts#L431-L468
8. **Log record and the lock.** `compactSurfaceRegion` appends `compaction/start {compactionId, turn}`, then prepares, summarises (with a recovery waterfall), checks stability, appends `compaction/summary` and a replacing `user/message` (a `replace` surface op citing the shadowed seqs), then `compaction/end`. Any failure before the commit body appends `compaction/end` with an `error` field, so a failed attempt leaves a start/end pair and an unchanged surface. Typed failure classes: `busy`, `cancelled`, `changed`, `summary`, `commit`, `persistence`. The latest start with no later end is "unmatched" and blocks further compaction unless a later `session/end-seed` event exists, so an orphan left by a crash is released when the session is next seeded. Manual compaction requires an idle agent (`runMaintenance`) and flushes the store before resolving. If the summary body committed but `compaction/end` failed, the surface is already replaced but the lock stays open until `end-seed`; a second compaction would be refused as `busy` (downstream consequence not traced). If the closing append itself fails, the original error is replaced by the close error. 9. **Overflow recovery.** On `agent/request-error` with code `CONTEXT_WINDOW_EXCEEDED`, up to `maxOverflowRetries` (default 1) per idle period: prune tool results (model-free pass, below), then compact ignoring the threshold and retention (`selectCompactableRange(..., 0)`, i.e. nothing kept verbatim), then return `{kind:'retry'}`. If the summary step fails after a prune already changed the surface, it still retries. If compaction fails, the signal is not aborted and no surface progress was made, the original request error is preserved. Retries reset when the agent goes idle or an assistant message lands. This is the staged fallback: prune, then compact with zero retention, then retry. Trust: traced end to end; not in the reviewed set. https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/index.ts#L213-L257 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/index.ts#L292-L313
10. **Model switch.** On `agent/request` with a different provider/model than the last header, the history is priced against the new model's window and compacted first; failure aborts the switch with `MODEL_SWITCH_COMPACTION_FAILED` and keeps the previous model. The input does not say what happens when the new model's window is too small for the summariser. Trust: unchecked. https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-basic/src/index.ts#L181-L203
11. **Images.** Over an image budget, the oldest images are replaced by placeholders and the request retried. https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-image-offload/src/index.ts#L27-L40

#### Pruning

- Model-free pruning of old tool output: once pressure qualifies, `pruneSession` runs first. Any tool result over 8,192 code points (`thresholdChars`) is replaced by head 4,096 + marker `[... tool result middle pruned ...]` + tail 1,024, as a `replace` op preceded by a `compaction/prune` shadow-price event. The full output is not stored for pruned results unless the spill plugin already saved it. Trust: traced end to end; unchecked. https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-tool-result-pruner/src/index.ts#L83-L122 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/compaction/compaction-tool-result-pruner/src/index.ts#L136-L182 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/bundle/base/cordis.patch.yml#L431-L438
- Spill at record time: a `tools/post-execute` listener counts tokens of a result; above `maxInlineTokens` (12,500 in the base bundle) it saves the full text through the spill store, keeps head and tail halves joined by `[...]`, and appends a notice with the stored locator and a retrieval hint. `read` results are exempt. Any failure keeps the content inline with a warning only, so a full disk or missing store lets an unbounded result into context (the pruner is then the next defence). Trust: checked in review. https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/spill/spill-policy/src/index.ts#L95-L131 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/bundle/base/cordis.patch.yml#L419-L425 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/spill/spill-policy/src/notice.ts#L22-L28

#### Instruction files

- Candidates: `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.local.md`, `CLAUDE.local.md` (names only). Sources in precedence order: user-global `AGENTS.md` in the DSH home, then the shared agents home; then for each directory from the project root (a `.git` marker) down to cwd, all candidates present. Files with identical trimmed content collapse. A per-file read cap `maxSourceBytes` of 1 MiB (larger files silently skipped); a render budget `maxBytes` of 65,536 per chain, with truncation or omission announced in the text ("Workspace instruction budget N bytes: ..."). A source TODO (`TODO(total-instruction-read-bound)`) admits there is no aggregate bound on bytes read across many files. Trust: checked in review. https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/context/agent-instructions/src/config.ts#L12-L13 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/context/agent-instructions/src/files.ts#L336-L363 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/context/agent-instructions/src/files.ts#L387-L414 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/context/agent-instructions/src/render.ts#L303-L312 , https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/fcc0ad462172522aaf5c93a7b2e01f6b9f0b0159/packages/bundle/base/cordis.patch.yml#L307-L310
- Delivery: not in the system prompt. A user message is folded into the first request's claimed batch. After that, `read`, `write` and `edit` successes in a subdirectory project that directory's nested instruction files, and changed or removed files, into the inbox for the next step. A file that vanishes between stat and read is skipped. ### goose

Repository `aaif-goose/goose`, pinned `14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892`. Rust. The loop is a stateless ordered pipeline of operations, each contributing tools, prompt parts and turn-context parts; compaction is two operations in that list (`CompactionOperation`, `ToolPairCompactionOperation`), skipped when the provider manages its own context (ACP and CLI providers). Sources: `goose.agents.md` (3.1, 3.3), `goose.sessions.md` (compaction storage), `goose.safety.md` (shell spill).

#### Prompt assembly and cache

- One template plus keyed extras, rebuilt from scratch before every inference call. The base is the minijinja template `system.md` (39 lines). Everything else is appended as keyed "extras" under `# Additional Instructions:`, joined by blank lines. `build_parts` sorts extensions by name, strips Unicode tag characters from extension instructions and every extra, renders the override or `system.md`, then appends hints. Extensions and tools are sorted by name explicitly for prompt-cache stability across sessions. `system.md` has no per-model branching. The date helper is rounded to the hour "so that prompt cache can be used" but `system.md` does not use it; time travels in the turn-context message instead. Trust: checked in review only for neighbouring findings; this one traced end to end, unchecked. https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/state_machine/inference_preparation.rs#L55-L63 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/prompt_manager.rs#L111-L172 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/prompt_manager.rs#L182-L196 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/prompt_manager.rs#L199-L206
- Template overrides: nine named templates (`system.md`, `compaction.md`, `compaction_summary.md`, `subagent_system.md`, `apps_create.md`, `apps_iterate.md`, `permission_judge.md`, `tiny_model_system.md`, `session_name.md`). `render_template` reads `<config_dir>/prompts/<name>` if present, else the embedded copy, on every render. A broken user template fails rendering with no fallback to the builtin; `permission_judge.md` is also overridable. Trust: unchecked. https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/prompt_template.rs#L9-L46 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/prompt_template.rs#L106-L132
- Extension instructions: one `extension:<name>` part per extension plus an `extensions` header; none at all in Code Mode; with tool deferral the header changes and an `mcp_tools` part lists deferred tool names. MCP server instructions are inserted verbatim (only Unicode tags stripped) with no size cap. Trust: unchecked. https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/state_machine/ops_toolcalling.rs#L931-L985
- Volatile state in a turn-context message ("MOIM"). Each turn a hidden agent-only user-role message `<turn-context>` is appended after the request messages with the current time (minute resolution), the working directory, extension-contributed parts (`get_moim`, platform extensions only) and a `<turn-budget>` part once half of `max_turns` is used. It is persisted so later requests within the turn reuse identical bytes, and skipped when the context limit is under 32,000 tokens. The `tom` ("Top Of Mind") platform extension feeds it from `GOOSE_MOIM_MESSAGE_TEXT` and `GOOSE_MOIM_MESSAGE_FILE`, each capped at 65,536 bytes. Compaction carries the latest turn-context message forward and drops older ones. Volatile data sits at the tail, so the system prefix stays cacheable. Trust: unchecked (MOIM not in the reviewed set; the Round 1 table row on the 64 KB cap is marked confirmed by the author). https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/moim.rs#L6-L34 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/moim.rs#L92-L134 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/platform_extensions/tom.rs#L65-L88 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/state_machine/inference_preparation.rs#L64-L84 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/context_mgmt/mod.rs#L222-L239
- Context report: `build_context_report` re-runs the same collection path against a stand-in next prompt and returns token counts per category (system prompt, extension instructions, additional instructions, turn context, tool definitions, compaction summary, messages) and whether auto-compaction is pending. Trust: unchecked. https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/context_report.rs#L77-L200

#### Compaction

1. **Trigger.** Threshold `GOOSE_AUTO_COMPACT_THRESHOLD` (default 0.8), lowered so it never exceeds `GOOSE_AUTO_COMPACT_TOKEN_LIMIT` (default 225,000) divided by the context limit, i.e. `min(0.8 x window, 225,000 tokens)`. A value <= 0.0 or >= 1.0 disables proactive compaction. Checked before every inference, only when the conversation does not end on an assistant message. Skipped entirely for providers that manage their own context. Trust: checked in review. https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/context_mgmt/mod.rs#L21-L53 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose-context-management/src/lib.rs#L32-L32 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/services.rs#L778-L785 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/state_machine/proactive_compaction.rs#L25-L43 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/agents/state_machine_builder.rs#L167-L181
2. **Size measurement.** The last provider-reported `total_tokens` plus a local estimate for tool results not yet reported; when no usage exists, a local tiktoken `o200k_base` count of all agent-visible messages. The check uses the previous inference's total, so one huge tool result between checks can overshoot (the docs admit this). The window comes from provider metadata, `GOOSE_CONTEXT_LIMIT` and canonical model data; how it is resolved for custom providers was not traced. Trust: checked in review. https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/token_counter.rs#L216-L216
3. **Summariser model.** The session's own provider and model (`GooseCompactionModel` wraps the session provider); `complete_one_shot` forces thinking off and disables prompt-cache writes. There is no separate cheap compaction model. The input says the summary call sends the whole flattened transcript as a system prompt (see 4), so it does not reuse the main request's prefix; the disabled cache write is the opposite of a cache-preserving request. Trust: traced end to end, unchecked (summariser format was in the "unchecked remainder"). https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/context_mgmt/mod.rs#L324-L347 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose/src/model_config.rs#L127-L151
4. **Prompt and input.** The history is flattened to `[role]: text` lines (thinking dropped, tool calls as `tool_request(name): {json}`, tool results as text only, images as `[image: mime]`) and placed in a system prompt (`compaction.md`) asking for working notes: a short `<analysis>` scratchpad, then a JSON block with `user_intent`, `technical_concepts`, `files` (path, summary, key_code), `errors_and_fixes` (verbatim error text), `problem_solving`, `user_messages`, `pending_tasks`, `current_work`, `next_step`. The user message is a fixed one-liner. The reply is parsed leniently and re-rendered by `compaction_summary.md` into markdown; if parsing fails the raw text is kept. Both templates are user-overridable. All tool results are sent (up to the staged removal below). https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose-context-management/src/summarize.rs#L124-L182 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose-context-management/src/prompts/compaction.md#L1-L45 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose-context-management/src/format.rs#L4-L83 , https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/14bf51817b79f6e98ca73c6eda61c11d9d5cc892/crates/goose-context-management/src/summarize.rs#L77-L93
5. **Summariser-window-too-small: staged tool-result removal.** If the summariser itself overflows its context it retries with tool responses removed from the middle outwards at 0%, 10%, 20%, 50%, 100% of tool-response messages. With no tool responses left to remove it fails fast with a message suggesting a larger window, fewer extensions or a new session. This is the only project in this group with a staged fallback inside the summary request. Appended, agent-only: the summary (role User, flagged `compaction_summary`), an assistant continuation line ("Your context was compacted ... Do not mention that you read a summary"; three variants: manual, conversation-end, mid-tool-loop), the most recent agent-visible, non-turn-context, text-only user message (text copied verbatim, images dropped; manual `/compact` does not preserve it), and the latest turn-context event. 7. **Overflow recovery (reactive, same pipeline step).** A provider error is persisted as an assistant message with an error kind; inference does not apply while the conversation ends in an error. The compaction operation, which precedes inference, sees a trailing `ContextLengthExceeded`, hides that error from the agent, compacts, and the loop reruns inference. It stops once more than `MAX_CONTEXT_ERROR_COMPACTIONS` (2) earlier hidden context errors exist in the turn (the fourth overflow in a turn gives up), after which `ExitOnErrorOperation` yields the error to the user. The overflowed request is replayed after the history is replaced by a summary, not truncated. If summarisation itself fails the user sees "Ran into this error trying to compact ...
